docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:140:     A concurrent worker renewal (`RenewAttemptLease`) or completion commit (`CommitAttempt`) may have executed between the unlocked candidate scan and row lock acquisition. Furthermore, the only authoritative grace check is evaluated inside the locks: if coordinator startup or outage grace is active, the sweeper skips candidate processing cleanly with zero state mutation. docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:141:     If the coordinator is in a grace window, OR if `attempts.state != 'RUNNING'`, OR if `attempts.lease_expires_at >= clock_timestamp()`, the attempt was concurrently committed, renewed, or protected by grace. The sweeper rolls back or skips the candidate cleanly with zero state mutation. (Accepted by owner).
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:166:#### Restart and Outage Grace Design
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:173:  2. *Option B (Outage Grace Window)*: Sweeper suppresses reaping following storage reconnection.
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:177:  2. **Database Outage Grace Window (`MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW`, default 30s)**: When the coordinator detects that the database has recovered from an outage, the sweeper suppresses reaping passes for 30 seconds. (Accepted by owner).
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:178:- **Outage Grace Detection Mechanism & Cooldown (D-R3-01)**:
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:179:  The sweeper opens an outage grace window of MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW (30 s) at the first successful tick after either (a) any failed sweeper tick, or (b) a database-time gap over 3x the sweep interval between consecutive successful ticks. A lock timeout or SKIP LOCKED skip on a single candidate is not a failed tick. After a window ends, a cooldown of the same length (30 s) begins, during which no new window opens and reaping proceeds, so suppression of reaping never exceeds one window length. The only authoritative grace check is the one inside the sweeper transaction lock.
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:183:  - **Validation Rule**: Both `MINICLOUD_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD` and `MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW` MUST be greater than or equal to the attempt lease duration (`MINICLOUD_LEASE_DURATION`, default `30s`). If either grace window is configured strictly less than the lease duration (e.g., `10s < 30s`), config validation rejects the startup with `ErrInvalidGraceConfiguration`.
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:186:  - In `TestSecurity_PartD_Outage_MidDispatchAndMidCommit`, the database container is stopped for 1.5s. Outage grace protects the attempt while the worker retries its commit upon database restoration.
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:188:  Coordinator-side grace periods must be paired with worker-side delivery resilience. If a task completes execution during a coordinator or database outage, the worker's result delivery loop (`internal/worker/client.go:324-355`) retries `ReportResult` with capped jittered backoff (500ms base to 5.0s cap) and concurrently continues issuing lease renewals every 10s until the coordinator recovers. This ensures that in scenarios with extended coordinator downtime (e.g. 45s), work that finished mid-downtime is not discarded by the worker before the coordinator and its startup grace window become available.
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:190:  - Zero existing test assertions are weakened. Existing integration tests run with restarts/outages of 200ms–1.5s, well within the 30s grace window.
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:191:  - Candidates for Owner Approval: If future tests explicitly configure short micro-leases (e.g. 500ms lease) to test fast expiry, they must explicitly configure `MINICLOUD_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD=0s` or wait for the grace window to elapse. Expanding `executeAndReport` from 5 attempts to a 60-second time-bounded loop with capped jittered backoff and ongoing lease renewal is an additive durability improvement submitted for owner approval in Slice 4c.
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:199:  - `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_CooldownPreventsConsecutiveGrace` (planned test).

docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:251:- **Restart & Outage Resilience**: Startup and outage grace periods prevent mass expiration of healthy jobs across coordinator restarts and database blips.
docs/adr/0015-retry-policy-and-failure-classification.md:84:  - **Consequence for Prolonged Outages**: Each lost attempt costs approximately lease duration (~30s) + up to one sweeper tick (~5s) + backoff delay. Repeated losses consume the `max_attempts` budget. While coordinator startup and database outage grace windows protect surviving workers during control-plane downtime, grace does not protect against worker-to-coordinator network partitions; prolonged partitions will exhaust `max_attempts` and transition the job to `FAILED`.
docs/adr/0016-cancellation-semantics.md:157:- **Deterministic Bounded Resolution**: Bounded 30s lease sweeps ensure `CANCEL_REQUESTED` always resolves to a terminal state within at most one lease duration (30 seconds) plus sweeper cadence, plus up to one grace window (30 seconds) if an outage happened, even if an uncooperative task ignores context cancellation, without requiring a separate reaper timer.

docs/architecture.md:89:If a query exceeds `statement_timeout` or waits longer than `lock_timeout`, PostgreSQL cancels the statement with SQLSTATE `57014` (`query_canceled`) or `55P03` (`lock_not_available`). The coordinator API error mapper maps these cancellation codes into HTTP 503 `SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE` with header `Retry-After: 1` and an opaque, sanitized JSON body (`{"error":{"code":"SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE","message":"service temporarily unavailable"}}`), preventing slow queries or lock contention from exhausting connection pool capacity (SEC-008). Operators can tune `MINICLOUD_DB_QUERY_TIMEOUT` if legitimate queries require more headroom.

The sweeper opens an outage grace window of MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW (30 s) at the first successful tick after either (a) any failed sweeper tick, or (b) a database-time gap over 3x the sweep interval between consecutive successful ticks. ### [G2e-adr-tests]
$ sed -n 174,200p docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md
```
  3. *Option C (Dynamic Lease Extension in DB)*: Shifting `lease_expires_at` forward by measured downtime.
- **Accepted Recommendation**: A dual grace period mechanism:
  1. **Coordinator Startup Grace Period (`MINICLOUD_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD`, default 30s)**: On coordinator boot or restart, the sweeper suppresses reaping passes until 30 seconds have elapsed on process/startup time. This gives surviving workers a full lease window to reconnect, auto-re-register, and execute at least 3 renewal attempts (renewal interval 10s) or commit completed results before any attempt can be reaped. The only authoritative grace check is inside the sweeper transaction lock; any earlier pre-check is advisory. (Accepted by owner).
  2. **Database Outage Grace Window (`MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW`, default 30s)**: When the coordinator detects that the database has recovered from an outage, the sweeper suppresses reaping passes for 30 seconds. (Accepted by owner).
- **Outage Grace Detection Mechanism & Cooldown (D-R3-01)**:
  The sweeper opens an outage grace window of MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW (30 s) at the first successful tick after either (a) any failed sweeper tick, or (b) a database-time gap over 3x the sweep interval between consecutive successful ticks. A lock timeout or SKIP LOCKED skip on a single candidate is not a failed tick. After a window ends, a cooldown of the same length (30 s) begins, during which no new window opens and reaping proceeds, so suppression of reaping never exceeds one window length. The only authoritative grace check is the one inside the sweeper transaction lock.
- **Configuration Names & Validation Rules**:
  - `MINICLOUD_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD`: Time to suppress sweeper reaping after coordinator boot (default: `30s`).
  - `MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW`: Time to suppress sweeper reaping after database recovery (default: `30s`).
  - **Validation Rule**: Both `MINICLOUD_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD` and `MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW` MUST be greater than or equal to the attempt lease duration (`MINICLOUD_LEASE_DURATION`, default `30s`). If either grace window is configured strictly less than the lease duration (e.g., `10s < 30s`), config validation rejects the startup with `ErrInvalidGraceConfiguration`.
- **Interaction with Phase 3 Tests**:
  - In `TestPostgresWiring_CoordinatorRestartRecovery`, Coordinator 1 is killed and Coordinator 2 starts up while a worker executes an 800ms task. With startup grace (30s), Coordinator 2's sweeper does not reap the attempt; the surviving worker commits with HTTP 200 COMMITTED, exactly preserving the Phase 3 contract.
  - In `TestSecurity_PartD_Outage_MidDispatchAndMidCommit`, the database container is stopped for 1.5s. Outage grace protects the attempt while the worker retries its commit upon database restoration.
- **Worker-Side Result Delivery Pairing During Outages**:
  Coordinator-side grace periods must be paired with worker-side delivery resilience. If a task completes execution during a coordinator or database outage, the worker's result delivery loop (`internal/worker/client.go:324-355`) retries `ReportResult` with capped jittered backoff (500ms base to 5.0s cap) and concurrently continues issuing lease renewals every 10s until the coordinator recovers. This ensures that in scenarios with extended coordinator downtime (e.g. 45s), work that finished mid-downtime is not discarded by the worker before the coordinator and its startup grace window become available.
- **Impact on Existing Tests & Owner Approval Candidates**:
  - Zero existing test assertions are weakened. Existing integration tests run with restarts/outages of 200ms–1.5s, well within the 30s grace window.
  - Candidates for Owner Approval: If future tests explicitly configure short micro-leases (e.g. 500ms lease) to test fast expiry, they must explicitly configure `MINICLOUD_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD=0s` or wait for the grace window to elapse. Expanding `executeAndReport` from 5 attempts to a 60-second time-bounded loop with capped jittered backoff and ongoing lease renewal is an additive durability improvement submitted for owner approval in Slice 4c.
- **Planned Grace Verification Tests**:
  - `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_OutageGraceDetection_SuppressesReap` (planned test).
  - `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_StartupGrace_SuppressesReap` (planned test).
  - `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_FailedTick_EntersGrace` (planned test).
  - `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_InterTickGapExceedsThreshold_EntersGrace` (planned test).
  - `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_InterTickGapBelowThreshold_EntersNoGrace` (planned test).
  - `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_CandidateLockTimeout_EntersNoGrace` (planned test).
  - `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_CooldownPreventsConsecutiveGrace` (planned test).
  - `TestPostgresRepository_DatabaseStopStart_WorkerRenewsSuccessfully` (planned test).
(exit 0)
```

```
      - 403 `WORKER_MISMATCH` or 409 (`JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`, `LEASE_EXPIRED`, `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED`): terminal rejection. Immediately abort task, cancel context, and do not report result.
      - 404: inspect body. If recognized not-found code (`JOB_NOT_FOUND`, `ATTEMPT_NOT_FOUND`, `WORKER_NOT_FOUND`), terminal rejection: abort task and do not report result. If missing or unrecognized error envelope (old coordinator), log warning once, disable renewals for this attempt, keep running task to completion, and report result via `POST /result`.
      - 400 (`INVALID_REQUEST`, `WORKER_TASK_UNSUPPORTED`): log error, stop renewing, keep running task to completion, report result via `POST /result`.
      - 5xx / transport error: transient failure. Keep running task, retry renewal next interval until lease expiry.
    - Worker result delivery (R-11): 60-second bounded retry loop with exponential backoff on transport errors and ongoing concurrent lease renewals; immediate exit on 4xx terminal rejections (`JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`, `LEASE_EXPIRED`, `WORKER_MISMATCH`, deterministic 400 `INVALID_REQUEST`, 404).
  - Coordinator Service (`internal/coordinator`):
    - Implement `StartLeaseSweeper(ctx, interval)` ticking every 5s (`MINICLOUD_SWEEPER_INTERVAL`).
    - Outage detection (R-09): The sweeper opens an outage grace window of MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW (30 s) at the first successful tick after either (a) any failed sweeper tick, or (b) a database-time gap over 3x the sweep interval between consecutive successful ticks. A lock timeout or SKIP LOCKED skip on a single candidate is not a failed tick. After a window ends, a cooldown of the same length (30 s) begins, during which no new window opens and reaping proceeds, so suppression of reaping never exceeds one window length. The only authoritative grace check is the one inside the sweeper transaction lock.
- **Exit Criteria**:
  - Long-running jobs (> 30s) maintain continuous lease renewals and commit successfully.
  - Cancellation delivered exclusively via renewal response within 10s.
  - Result finished during a 45s coordinator/database outage commits within 60s delivery bound once coordinator/database recovers.
  - Worker receiving lease loss aborts task and suppresses result reporting.
  - Terminating a worker process triggers automated lease expiry and job reclamation.
  - Worker renewal handles missing endpoints (old coordinator) and 400 errors without failing the executing task.
  - **Mutation Proofs (Mandatory)**:
    - Mutating worker renewal response handler to ignore `cancel_requested: true` must cause cooperative cancellation test to fail.
    - Disabling worker abort upon renewal rejection must cause worker zombie isolation test to fail.
    - Mutating outage gap threshold or cooldown must cause outage grace recovery tests to fail.
- **Gating Tests**:
  - `TestAPI_RenewLease_SuccessAndStaleRejection` (planned test)
  - `TestAgent_PeriodicLeaseRenewal_Success` (planned test)
  - `TestAgent_LeaseRenewal_ReceivesCancelRequested` (planned test)
  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_ReclaimsAbandonedAttempt` (planned test)
  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_InterTickGapExceedsThreshold_EntersGrace` (planned test)
  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_InterTickGapBelowThreshold_EntersNoGrace` (planned test)
  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_FailedTick_EntersGrace` (planned test)
  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_CandidateLockTimeout_EntersNoGrace` (planned test)
  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_CooldownPreventsConsecutiveGrace` (planned test)
  - `TestWorker_Client_RenewOldCoordinatorGracefulDegradation` (planned test)
  - `TestWorker_Client_Renew400KeepsRunning` (planned test)
(exit 0)

946	func (r *Repository) CommitAttempt(ctx context.Context, jobID, attemptID, leaseToken, workerID string, outcome domain.AttemptOutcome) (*domain.Job, error) {
947		tx, err := r.pool.Begin(ctx)
948		if err != nil {
949			return nil, mapPgError(err)
950		}
951		defer tx.Rollback(ctx)
952	
953		// Step 1: Lock Job row FOR UPDATE
954		jobQuery := `
955			SELECT id, task_type, payload, state, max_attempts, timeout_seconds, current_attempt_id, created_at, updated_at
956			FROM jobs
957			WHERE id = $1
958			FOR UPDATE;
959		`
960		var (
961			job              domain.Job
962			jobStateStr      string
963			currentAttemptID *string
964		)
965		err = tx.QueryRow(ctx, jobQuery, jobID).Scan(
966			&job.ID,
967			&job.TaskType,
968			&job.Payload,
969			&jobStateStr,
970			&job.MaxAttempts,
971			&job.TimeoutSeconds,
972			&currentAttemptID,
973			&job.CreatedAt,
974			&job.UpdatedAt,
975		)
976		if err != nil {
977			if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
978				return nil, domain.ErrJobNotFound
979			}
980			return nil, mapPgError(err)
981		}
982		job.State = domain.JobState(jobStateStr)
983		if job.State.IsTerminal() {
984			return nil, domain.ErrJobAlreadyTerminal
985		}
986		if currentAttemptID == nil || *currentAttemptID != attemptID {
987			return nil, domain.ErrStaleAttempt
988		}
989		job.CurrentAttemptID = *currentAttemptID
990	
991		// Step 2: Lock Attempt row FOR UPDATE
992		attemptQuery := `
993			SELECT id, job_id, attempt_no, worker_id, lease_token_hash, state, started_at
994			FROM attempts
995			WHERE id = $1
996			FOR UPDATE;
997		`
998		var (
999			attID          string
1000			attJobID       string
1001			attNo          int
1002			attWorkerID    string
1003			leaseTokenHash string
1004			attStateStr    string
1005			startedAt      time.Time
1006		)
1007		err = tx.QueryRow(ctx, attemptQuery, attemptID).Scan(
1008			&attID,
1009			&attJobID,
1010			&attNo,
1011			&attWorkerID,
1012			&leaseTokenHash,
1013			&attStateStr,
1014			&startedAt,
1015		)
1016		if err != nil {
1017			if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
1018				return nil, domain.ErrAttemptNotFound
1019			}
1020			return nil, mapPgError(err)
1021		}
1022	
1023		if attStateStr != string(domain.AttemptRunning) {
1024			return nil, domain.ErrStaleAttempt
1025		}
1026	
1027		// Constant-time hash comparison in Go to prevent timing leaks
1028		providedHash := domain.HashLeaseToken(leaseToken)
1029		if subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(leaseTokenHash), []byte(providedHash)) != 1 {
1030			return nil, domain.ErrStaleAttempt
1031		}
1032	
1033		if attWorkerID != workerID {
1034			return nil, &domain.WorkerMismatchError{
1035				AssignedWorker:  attWorkerID,
1036				ReportingWorker: workerID,
1037			}
1038		}
1039	
1040		// Step 3: Determine outcomes
1041		var (
1042			targetAttemptState string
1043			targetJobState     domain.JobState
1044			jobEventType       domain.JobEventType
1045		)
1046		if outcome.ExitCode == 0 && outcome.Error == "" {
1047			targetAttemptState = string(domain.AttemptCompleted)
1048			targetJobState = domain.StateSucceeded
1049			jobEventType = domain.JobEventCompleted
1050		} else {
1051			targetAttemptState = string(domain.AttemptFailed)
1052			targetJobState = domain.StateFailed
1053			jobEventType = domain.JobEventFailed
1054		}
1055	
1056		// Step 4: Update attempt
1057		updateAttemptQuery := `
1058			UPDATE attempts
1059			SET state = $1, finished_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, exit_code = $2, output = $3, error = $4
1060			WHERE id = $5;
1061		`
1062		_, err = tx.Exec(ctx, updateAttemptQuery,
1063			targetAttemptState,
1064			outcome.ExitCode,
1065			outcome.Output,
1066			outcome.Error,
1067			attemptID,
1068		)
1069		if err != nil {
1070			return nil, mapPgError(err)
1071		}
1072	
1073		// Step 5: Update job
1074		fromState := string(job.State)
1075		toState := string(targetJobState)

### [G2a-inmem-commit]

570	func (r *InMemoryRepository) CommitAttempt(ctx context.Context, jobID, attemptID, leaseToken, workerID string, outcome domain.AttemptOutcome) (*domain.Job, error) {
571		r.mu.Lock()
572		defer r.mu.Unlock()
573	
574		job, exists := r.jobs[jobID]
575		if !exists {
576			return nil, domain.ErrJobNotFound
577		}
578		if job.State.IsTerminal() {
579			return nil, domain.ErrJobAlreadyTerminal
580		}
581		if job.CurrentAttemptID != attemptID {
582			return nil, domain.ErrStaleAttempt
583		}
584	
585		att, exists := r.attempts[attemptID]
586		if !exists {
587			return nil, domain.ErrAttemptNotFound
588		}
589		if att.State != domain.AttemptRunning {
590			return nil, domain.ErrStaleAttempt
591		}
592	
593		// Constant-time compare SHA-256 hash of provided lease token
594		providedHashHex := domain.HashLeaseToken(leaseToken)
595		if subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(att.LeaseTokenHash), []byte(providedHashHex)) != 1 {
596			return nil, domain.ErrStaleAttempt
597		}
598	
599		// Verify worker match
600		if att.WorkerID != workerID {
601			return nil, &domain.WorkerMismatchError{
602				AssignedWorker:  att.WorkerID,
603				ReportingWorker: workerID,
604			}
605		}
606	
607		now := time.Now().UTC()
608		att.FinishedAt = &now
609		att.ExitCode = outcome.ExitCode
610		att.Output = outcome.Output
611		att.Error = outcome.Error
612	
613		var targetState domain.JobState
614		var eventType domain.JobEventType
615		if outcome.ExitCode == 0 && outcome.Error == "" {
616			att.State = domain.AttemptCompleted
617			targetState = domain.StateSucceeded
618			eventType = domain.JobEventCompleted
619		} else {
620			att.State = domain.AttemptFailed
621			targetState = domain.StateFailed
622			eventType = domain.JobEventFailed
623		}
624	
625		fromState := job.State
626		if err := job.TransitionTo(targetState); err != nil {
627			return nil, err
628		}
629		job.Result = &domain.Result{
630			ExitCode: outcome.ExitCode,
631			Output:   outcome.Output,
632			Error:    outcome.Error,
633		}
634		job.UpdatedAt = now
635	
636		r.recordEventLocked(&domain.JobEvent{
637			JobID:     jobID,
638			AttemptID: &attemptID,
639			EventType: eventType,
640			FromState: &fromState,
641			ToState:   &targetState,
642			CreatedAt: now,
643			Details: map[string]any{
644				"worker_id": workerID,
645				"exit_code": outcome.ExitCode,
646			},
647		})
648	
649		copied := *job
650		if job.Result != nil {
651			res := *job.Result
652			copied.Result = &res
653		}
654		return &copied, nil
655	}
656	
657	// CancelJob atomically cancels a queued or running job and records an audit event.
658	func (r *InMemoryRepository) CancelJob(ctx context.Context, jobID string) (*domain.Job, error) {
659		r.mu.Lock()
660		defer r.mu.Unlock()
(exit 0)

### [G2a-adr16-sec1]
$ awk 'NR>=40 && NR<=60 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/adr/0016-cancellation-semantics.md; echo ----; awk 'NR>=108 && NR<=130 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/adr/0016-cancellation-semantics.md
```
40	## Decision
41	
42	### 1. Behavior Changes Introduced by Phase 4 Design
43	The Phase 4 design introduces four concrete behavioral changes to the existing cancellation implementation:
44	1. **Idempotent Duplicate Cancel on `CANCEL_REQUESTED`**:
45	   - *Current Behavior*: A second call to `CancelJob` while `job.state == 'CANCEL_REQUESTED'` falls into the `else` block, moving the job prematurely to `CANCELLED` while the worker may still be executing the attempt. When the worker later commits, the commit is rejected as terminal (`JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`) without closing the attempt, permanently leaking worker capacity (SEC-022).
46	   - *New Behavior*: A duplicate cancel on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job is an idempotent no-op performed directly in the repository `CancelJob` method under the job row lock (in PostgreSQL) or mutex (in-memory). It returns the existing job state with HTTP 200 `CANCEL_REQUESTED` (with the same body as the first cancellation request) and does not insert duplicate audit events or invoke FSM transitions. In Slice 4a, `CommitAttempt` on a terminal job closes the attempt and frees the worker capacity slot in the same transaction as the rejection, writing a diagnostic event and returning 409 `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`.
47	2. **Cancellation During `RETRYING`**:
48	   - When a job is in `RETRYING` awaiting backoff, `CancelJob` immediately transitions it to `CANCELLED` (terminal), clears `not_before`, and removes it from dispatch eligibility.
49	3. **Running Attempt Resolution via Lease / Sweeper Path**:
50	   - The active attempt is resolved through the lease/renewal path: either the worker receives `cancel_requested: true` on its renewal and reports cooperative cancellation, or the worker commits valid completion, or the coordinator lease sweeper reaps the expired lease and transitions `CANCEL_REQUESTED -> CANCELLED`.
51	4. **Failure Commit on CANCEL_REQUESTED Resolves Job to CANCELLED**:
52	   - Today `internal/storage/inmemory.go:619-622` moves a job to `FAILED` upon failure commit. Under the new behavior, any commit on a job in `CANCEL_REQUESTED` reporting a failure (non-zero exit code or error string, including retryable failures) transitions the attempt to `FAILED` and the job to `CANCELLED` (cancel wins, never `RETRYING` or `FAILED`). Planned test: `TestConformance_CommitAttempt_FailureOnCancelRequestedYieldsCancelledJob` (planned test).
53	
54	### 2. State Differentiation by Lifecycle Phase
55	When `POST /v1/jobs/{job_id}/cancel` is invoked:
56	1. **If Job is in `QUEUED`**:
57	   - Transition: `QUEUED` -> `CANCELLED` immediately.
58	   - Dispatch eligibility: Job is marked terminal in repository and skipped by subsequent `DispatchNextJob` pulls.
59	   - API Response: `200 OK` with `state: "CANCELLED"`, `message: "cancellation processed"`.
60	2. **If Job is in `RETRYING`**:
----
108	
109	### 5. Resolution of Distributed Races
110	
111	#### Race 1: Cancellation vs Completion
112	- **Scenario**: Client submits cancellation while worker has finished computation and is calling `CommitAttempt`.
113	- **Resolution**:
114	  - `CommitAttempt` locks the `jobs` row `FOR UPDATE`.
115	  - In MiniCloud's FSM (`internal/domain/transition.go:21`), `CANCEL_REQUESTED` explicitly permits transitions to `SUCCEEDED`, `FAILED`, and `CANCELLED`.
116	  - **Success Outcome**: If the worker successfully produced a valid completion (`outcome.ExitCode == 0 && outcome.Error == ""`):
117	    - Attempt transitions: `AttemptCompleted` (`"COMPLETED"`).
118	    - Job transitions: `CANCEL_REQUESTED` -> `SUCCEEDED`.
119	    - Result is stored in `results`.
120	    - Event: `JobEventCompleted` (`"JOB_COMPLETED"`).
121	    - This preserves today's verified behavior in `internal/storage/inmemory.go:615-618` and `internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:1046-1049`.
122	  - **Failure / Cancel Outcome**: Any commit on a job in `CANCEL_REQUESTED` with a failure (non-zero exit code or error string, including retryable failures):
123	    - Closes the attempt with the outcome reported by the worker (attempt `state = 'FAILED'`).
124	    - Job transitions: `CANCEL_REQUESTED` -> `CANCELLED` (terminal; cancel wins, never `RETRYING` from `CANCEL_REQUESTED`).
125	    - Event: `JobEventCancelled` (`"JOB_CANCELLED"`).
126	
127	#### Race 2: Cancellation vs Lease Expiry (Zombie / Unresponsive Worker)
128	- **Scenario**: Job is in `CANCEL_REQUESTED`. Worker hangs in non-cooperative task (ignoring context cancellation), loses network connectivity, or dies before reporting outcome.
129	- **Resolution**:
130	  - The renewal response on `CANCEL_REQUESTED` did not extend the lease, and the worker ceased renewing. Therefore, the lease expires within at most one lease duration (30 seconds) from the last renewal.
(exit 0)

$ awk 'NR>=36 && NR<=56 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/state-machine.md
```
36	| **`FAILED`** | NO | NO | NO | NO | NO | NO | — | NO | NO |
37	| **`CANCELLED`** | NO | NO | NO | NO | NO | NO | NO | — | NO |
38	| **`REJECTED`** | NO | NO | NO | NO | NO | NO | NO | NO | — |
39	
40	*Note: In the domain FSM (`internal/domain/transition.go:4-33`), the diagonal is strictly `—` (no self-transitions). A duplicate cancel on `CANCEL_REQUESTED` is handled in the repository `CancelJob` under row lock as an idempotent no-op (a second cancel on `CANCEL_REQUESTED` returns the job unchanged without invoking an FSM state transition). Terminal states (`SUCCEEDED`, `FAILED`, `CANCELLED`, `REJECTED`) are strictly immutable. No transition away from a terminal state is permitted under any circumstances.*
41	
42	### Current Code Behavior vs Phase 4 Behavior Changes
43	- **Verified Current Cancellation Behavior**:
44	  Cancellation today behaves identically in `internal/storage/inmemory.go:658-700` and `internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:1135-1233`:
45	  1. Terminal job -> `domain.ErrJobAlreadyTerminal`.
46	  2. `QUEUED` -> `CANCELLED` (event `JOB_CANCELLED`).
47	  3. `RUNNING` -> `CANCEL_REQUESTED` (event `JOB_CANCEL_REQUESTED`), with the active attempt untouched.
48	  4. Any other non-terminal state (including `CANCEL_REQUESTED` and future `RETRYING`) -> falls through to `CANCELLED` (event `JOB_CANCELLED`).
49	  5. Workers learn nothing today (heartbeats carry static `{"status":"ACK"}`).
50	- **Correction of Prior Misdescription**: Prior design drafts incorrectly claimed that cancelling a `RUNNING` job moved it directly to `CANCELLED` and marked the attempt `FAILED`. That description was false. The code already sets `CANCEL_REQUESTED` on first call for `RUNNING` jobs and leaves the attempt in `RUNNING`.
51	- **Phase 4 Behavior Changes Introduced**:
52	  1. **Idempotent Duplicate Cancel on `CANCEL_REQUESTED`**: Currently, a second cancel on `CANCEL_REQUESTED` moves the job prematurely to `CANCELLED` while the worker may still be executing. Under Phase 4, the repository performs an idempotent duplicate-cancel no-op under row lock returning the job in `CANCEL_REQUESTED` unchanged (API returns HTTP 200 with state `CANCEL_REQUESTED`, identical response body as the first cancel). The domain transition matrix is unchanged by this (`CANCEL_REQUESTED -> CANCEL_REQUESTED` remains invalid in the FSM).
53	  2. **Cancellation during `RETRYING`**: Cancelling a job in `RETRYING` stays an immediate transition to `CANCELLED`, clearing `not_before` and aborting scheduled retries.
54	  3. **Running Attempt Resolution via Lease / Commit Path**: The worker discovers cancellation via the renewal response (`cancel_requested: true`, unextended `lease_expires_at`). The worker cancels the task context, ceases renewals, and still reports its result. If the task ignores cancellation, the lease expires within one lease duration (30 s); the sweeper closes the attempt as `CANCELLED` and the job becomes `CANCELLED` (never `RETRYING`). Any commit on `CANCEL_REQUESTED` closes the attempt with the reported outcome (`COMPLETED` on success, `FAILED` on error/cancel) and moves the job: success moves `CANCEL_REQUESTED -> SUCCEEDED` (today's behavior in `internal/storage/inmemory.go:615-618` and `internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:1046-1049`, permitted by `internal/domain/transition.go:21`); failure moves `CANCEL_REQUESTED -> CANCELLED` (cancel wins, never `RETRYING`). The coordinator requires no special cancel acknowledgement wire signal.
55	  4. **Direct `RETRYING -> RUNNING` via Pull Dispatch**: Blueprint conceptual diagrams describe `RETRYING -> QUEUED` when a retry backoff timer elapses. In MiniCloud's implementation, transitioning `RETRYING -> RUNNING` directly via `repo.DispatchNextJob` (`SELECT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` where `(state = 'QUEUED' OR (state = 'RETRYING' AND not_before <= clock_timestamp()))`) eliminates unnecessary intermediate database writes and polling loops while preserving full auditability via `JobEventDispatched` (`from_state: 'RETRYING'`, `to_state: 'RUNNING'`). (Accepted by owner).
56	
(exit 0)

$ awk 'NR>=20 && NR<=245 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/phase4-plan.md
```
20	## 2. Proposed Sub-Slices
21	
22	### Slice 4a: Grace Design, Existing-Test Impact, Domain State Machine & In-Memory Parity
23	- **Grace Design & Rationale**:
24	  - Leases expire strictly on database time (`lease_expires_at = clock_timestamp() + 30s`).
25	  - Without grace, any coordinator restart or database outage lasting > 30s causes the background sweeper to mass-expire healthy running attempts to `LOST`, scheduling redundant retry attempts and causing duplicate execution.
26	  - This directly violates the Phase 3 recovery invariant verified in `TestPostgresWiring_CoordinatorRestartRecovery` (`tests/integration/postgres_wiring_test.go:167`) and `TestSecurity_PartD_Outage_MidDispatchAndMidCommit` (`tests/integration/part_d_security_test.go:794`), where surviving workers holding valid tasks successfully commit after the control plane recovers.
27	  - *Mechanism*: The sweeper enforces two grace conditions before sweeping:
28	    1. *Startup Grace Period*: Sweeper pauses reclamation for `MINICLOUD_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD` (30s) after coordinator boot, allowing surviving workers to issue renewals.
29	    2. *Database Outage Grace Window*: The sweeper opens an outage grace window of MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW (30 s) at the first successful tick after either (a) any failed sweeper tick, or (b) a database-time gap over 3x the sweep interval between consecutive successful ticks. A lock timeout or SKIP LOCKED skip on a single candidate is not a failed tick. After a window ends, a cooldown of the same length (30 s) begins, during which no new window opens and reaping proceeds, so suppression of reaping never exceeds one window length. The only authoritative grace check is the one inside the sweeper transaction lock.
30	- **Existing-Test Impact List**:
31	  1. *Cancellation Duplicate-Call Parity & SEC-022 Authorized Fix Scope*:
32	     - *Current Behavior*: Today, `internal/storage/inmemory.go:658-700` and `internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:1135-1233` behave identically: terminal job -> `ErrJobAlreadyTerminal`; `QUEUED` -> `CANCELLED`; `RUNNING` -> `CANCEL_REQUESTED` (attempt unmodified in `RUNNING`); and any subsequent non-terminal state (including `CANCEL_REQUESTED` and `RETRYING`) transitions to `CANCELLED` via fallback `else` branch.
33	     - *Phase 4 Behavior Change (SEC-022 Authorized Fix)*:
34	       - (a) A duplicate cancel on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job becomes an idempotent no-op returning HTTP 200 with state `CANCEL_REQUESTED` (same response body as the first cancellation request), preventing the job from prematurely terminating while the worker is actively running and preventing subsequent result commits from being rejected as terminal.
35	       - (b) `CommitAttempt` on a terminal job must close the attempt and free the worker capacity slot in the same transaction as the rejection, record a diagnostic event, and still return HTTP 409 `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`.
36	       - (c) The regression test is the audit probe `TestReviewP400_SEC022_DoubleCancelLeaksWorkerSlot` (audit probe, executed on `agent/claude/P4-00-review`; regression in slice 4a), to be cherry-picked from branch `agent/claude/P4-00-review` in slice 4a and moved into the normal test tree.
37	       - (d) Enforce Invariant 4 ("No Active Attempt After Terminal State") in `TestPostgresRepository_Concurrency_MixedOperationsStress` (`docs/fault-injection-plan.md:141`).
38	     - *Existing Test Inspection & Impact*:
39	       - The existing test `CancelJob_QueuedAndRunning` in `internal/storage/storagetest/conformance.go:299-363` (specifically line 335) asserts that calling `repo.CancelJob` a second time on a terminal `CANCELLED` job returns `domain.ErrJobAlreadyTerminal`.
40	       - The existing test `TestInMemoryRepository_SubmitJob_And_CancelJob_Events` in `internal/storage/inmemory_test.go:559` (specifically line 607) asserts that calling `repo.CancelJob` a second time on a terminal `CANCELLED` job returns `domain.ErrJobAlreadyTerminal`.
41	       - Slice 4a changes duplicate-cancel behavior on non-terminal `CANCEL_REQUESTED` jobs (the job remains in `CANCEL_REQUESTED` rather than prematurely jumping to `CANCELLED`), but changes zero HTTP status codes (HTTP 200 continues to be returned for all valid cancellation requests). No existing test in the test suite asserts a second-cancel response on `CANCEL_REQUESTED`.
42	       - *Impact*: Zero existing tests are broken by making duplicate cancel on `CANCEL_REQUESTED` an idempotent no-op returning HTTP 200.
43	       - *New Conformance Test*: `TestConformance_CancelJob_IdempotentCancelRequested` (planned test) will be added to `storagetest/conformance.go`.
44	  2. *FSM Transition Matrix Updates (`TestJobTransitionMatrix_AllPairs` in `internal/domain/transition_test.go`)*:
45	     - Exactly two matrix cells change validity (marked "candidate for owner approval"):
46	       - `RETRYING -> QUEUED`: was valid in initial matrix (`true`), becomes invalid (`false`) (candidate for owner approval; jobs in `RETRYING` do not transition back to `QUEUED`, they dispatch directly `RETRYING -> RUNNING` via `DispatchNextJob`).
47	       - `RETRYING -> RUNNING`: was invalid in initial matrix (`false`), becomes valid (`true`) (candidate for owner approval; direct dispatch).
48	     - Note: `CANCEL_REQUESTED -> CANCEL_REQUESTED` remains invalid in the domain FSM transition table (`internal/domain/transition.go:4-33`), so its cell does NOT flip. Duplicate cancel is handled in repository `CancelJob` under row lock as an idempotent no-op (returning the job in `CANCEL_REQUESTED` unchanged).
49	     - *Impact*: `TestJobTransitionMatrix_AllPairs` cell assertions for the two flipped cells are updated in Slice 4a.
50	  3. *HTTP API Cancel Status Code Parity (Unchanged from Today)*:
51	     - All cancellation requests for valid jobs (`QUEUED`, `RETRYING`, `RUNNING`, or duplicate cancels on `CANCEL_REQUESTED`) return HTTP 200 `OK`. Status codes are completely unchanged from today. There is no HTTP 202 `Accepted`, no ADR-0004 exception, and no `duplicate` response field.
52	     - Planned test: `TestAPI_CancelJob_SuccessReturns200` (planned test).
53	  4. *In-Memory Retry Dispatch & Coordinator Rewiring (R-05, D-R2-01 & R3-09)*:
54	     - `RETRYING` jobs dispatch through `repo.DispatchNextJob` in both modes. The memory-mode coordinator (`internal/coordinator/service.go:401-409`) is rewired to call `repo.DispatchNextJob` in slice 4a, achieving full dispatch parity with PostgreSQL.
55	     - *Coordinator Queue Operations & Constructor Fate*:
56	       - When DB dispatch replaces queue operations in Slice 4a, `SubmitJob`'s `s.queue.Enqueue` (`internal/coordinator/service.go:194-195`) and `CommitResult`'s `s.queue.Ack` (`internal/coordinator/service.go:512-513`) are removed.
57	       - `NewServiceWithQueue` (`internal/coordinator/service.go:71-72`) and its queue parameter (still passed in `tests/integration/multi_worker_batch_test.go:39-40`) are candidates for owner approval (propose deprecation/retirement or compatibility wrapper).
58	       - Callers constructing memory coordinators without queue assertions remain unaffected: `internal/coordinator/service_test.go:47`, `:274`, `internal/api/api_test.go:58`, `:588`, `tests/integration/coordinator_restart_test.go:67`, `:181`, `tests/integration/worker_liveness_test.go:43`.
59	     - *Integration Tests using Memory Driver*:
60	       - `tests/integration/local_core_test.go:25` and `tests/integration/multi_worker_batch_test.go:26`: use memory repository and coordinator. When `RETRYING` jobs exist, memory coordinator now properly claims them via `DispatchNextJob` without stranding them.
61	       - `internal/api/api_test.go:27`: uses memory coordinator. `ClaimWork` routes through `DispatchNextJob`, so dispatch behavior and attempt tracking match postgres repository.
62	     - *Existing-Test Impact on Coordinator Service Tests*:
63	       - Moving memory dispatch to `repo.DispatchNextJob` directly impacts coordinator tests in `internal/coordinator/service_test.go`:
64	         - Six tests use `spyQueue` (`internal/coordinator/service_test.go:338-381`) and assert queue `Ack`/`Release` calls; they are rewritten to assert observable domain outcomes (job state, attempt state, worker slot capacity) instead of queue method calls (each a candidate for owner approval):
65	           1. `TestService_Claim_JobNoLongerQueued_Dropped` (`internal/coordinator/service_test.go:386`) (candidate for owner approval)
66	           2. `TestService_Claim_JobNotQueuedAndWorkerUnhealthy_DroppedAndAckedNeverReleased` (`internal/coordinator/service_test.go:458`) (candidate for owner approval)
67	           3. `TestService_CommitResult_AckOnlyOnAccepted` (`internal/coordinator/service_test.go:575`) (candidate for owner approval)

72	  5. *Restart & Outage Recovery Tests*:

74	     - With the 30s startup and outage grace windows, surviving workers commit within the grace window, keeping test assertions valid without weakening any existing test assertion.
75	  6. *RETRYING Cancellation Conformance*:

Map/rename domain constants `AttemptState` in `internal/domain/state.go:22-27` (`EXPIRED` -> `LOST`, `CANCELED` -> `CANCELLED`).
80	  - Update `internal/domain`:

114	### Slice 4b: Schema Migration 000004 & PostgreSQL Repository Durable Implementation
115	- **Scope**:
116	  - Author embedded migration `000004_leases_retries_cancellation.sql` (proposed):
117	    - Columns: `attempts.lease_expires_at` (with temporary default for historical row backfill, dropped immediately after), `attempts.error_class` (strictly checked for `'PERMANENT'`, `'TRANSIENT'`, `'UNKNOWN'`, `'LEASE_EXPIRED'`), `attempts.last_error`, `jobs.not_before`, `jobs.cancel_requested_at`.
118	    - Indexes: `idx_attempts_lease_expiry`, `idx_jobs_retrying_dispatch`, and partial unique index `idx_job_events_unique_diagnostic_late_result` on `(job_id, (details->>'attempt_id')) WHERE event_type = 'DIAGNOSTIC_LATE_RESULT'`.
119	    - Check constraints: `chk_attempts_error_class`, `chk_attempts_last_error_size` using `NOT VALID` then `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT`.
120	  - Implement durable repository methods in `internal/storage/postgres.Repository`:
121	    - `RenewLease`: atomic transaction verifying attempt ownership, active `RUNNING` attempt (symmetric accept-until-swept rule), and constant-time token comparison.
122	    - `SweepExpiredLeases`: single-transaction lock-ordered candidate sweep (`jobs -> workers -> attempts -> results -> job_events`).
123	    - *Canonical Sweeper Transaction (R-08)*:
124	      1. Candidate scan (unlocked read against index `idx_attempts_lease_expiry`).
125	      2. Per-candidate lock order `jobs -> workers -> attempts`: `SELECT id, state, max_attempts FROM jobs WHERE id = $1 FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` (0 rows = skip and retry next tick), then workers and attempts rows with plain `FOR UPDATE` under PostgreSQL `lock_timeout` (default 5s). One grace check inside locks. `SKIP LOCKED` and `NOWAIT` are never combined.
126	      3. Sweeper state resolution rules (R-02):
127	         - If parent job is already terminal (`SUCCEEDED`, `FAILED`, `CANCELLED`): close attempt as `CANCELLED` (if job is `CANCELLED`) or `LOST` (otherwise), leave job state unchanged, free worker slot, and record `LEASE_EXPIRED` event.
128	         - If `CANCEL_REQUESTED`: close attempt as `CANCELLED`, transition job to `CANCELLED`, free worker slot, record `JOB_CANCELLED` event.
129	         - If `RUNNING` and `attempt_no < max_attempts`: close attempt as `LOST` (`error_class = 'LEASE_EXPIRED'`), set exponential backoff `not_before`, transition job to `RETRYING`, free worker slot, record `LEASE_EXPIRED` and `JOB_RETRYING` events.
130	         - If `RUNNING` and `attempt_no >= max_attempts`: close attempt as `LOST` (`error_class = 'LEASE_EXPIRED'`), transition job to `FAILED`, free worker slot, record `LEASE_EXPIRED` and `JOB_FAILED` events.
131	    - Update `DispatchNextJob`: query evaluates `WHERE (state = 'QUEUED' OR (state = 'RETRYING' AND not_before <= clock_timestamp()))` with direct transition to `RUNNING`.
132	    - Update `CommitAttempt`: enforce 409 evaluation order:
133	      1. Terminal job -> close attempt (strictly if still in `RUNNING`), free worker slot, record diagnostic event, and return 409 `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL` (the single stored result stands; an attempt already terminal is never overwritten). On `CANCEL_REQUESTED`, success moves attempt `COMPLETED` and job `SUCCEEDED` (`internal/storage/inmemory.go:615-618`, `internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:1046-1049`, `internal/domain/transition.go:21`); failure moves attempt `FAILED` and job `CANCELLED`.
134	      2. Attempt swept to `LOST` -> return 409 `LEASE_EXPIRED`.
135	      3. Superseded attempt or token mismatch -> return 409 `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED`.
136	    - Update `CancelJob`: idempotent no-op on `CANCEL_REQUESTED`, immediate `CANCELLED` on `RETRYING`.
137	  - Run shared conformance test suite against live PostgreSQL container.
138	- **Exit Criteria**:
139	  - Forward-only migration 000004 applies cleanly to fresh and migrated databases.
140	  - PostgreSQL repository passes all shared conformance tests under `-race`.
141	  - Zero deadlocks under concurrent mixed operations stress test (`-race -count=20`), with Invariant 4 (No Active Attempt After Terminal State, [fault-injection-plan.md](fault-injection-plan.md#L141)) strictly verified.
142	  - **Mutation Proofs (Mandatory)**:
143	    - Bypassing row lock or re-check in `SweepExpiredLeases` must fail `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_ConcurrentRenewalRecheck` (planned test).
144	    - Removing startup grace check in sweeper must cause `TestPostgresWiring_CoordinatorRestartGrace_SurvivingCommit` (planned test) to fail.
145	    - Mutating terminal job attempt closing in `CommitAttempt` must cause `TestPostgresRepository_CommitAttempt_TerminalJobClosesAttempt` (planned test) to fail.
146	- **Gating Tests**:
147	  - `TestMigrator_FreshMigrate`
148	  - `TestPostgresRepository_Conformance` (expanded with Phase 4 tests)
149	  - `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_CanonicalLockOrder` (planned test)
150	  - `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_ConcurrentRenewalRecheck` (planned test)
151	  - `TestPostgresRepository_CommitAttempt_TerminalJobClosesAttempt` (planned test)
152	  - `TestPostgresWiring_CoordinatorRestartGrace_SurvivingCommit` (planned test)
153	  - `TestPostgresRepository_Concurrency_MixedOperationsStress` (expanded with leases, cancels, and Invariant 4 check)
154	- **Risks & Mitigations**:
155	  - Sweeper lock contention with active workers. *Mitigation:* Small batches (LIMIT 50) and strict lock order `jobs -> workers -> attempts`.
156

159	### Slice 4c: HTTP API Protocol, Worker Lease Renewal & Coordinator Sweeper Wiring
160	- **Scope**:
161	  - HTTP API (`internal/api`):
162	    - Dedicated `POST /v1/jobs/{job_id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/renew` endpoint.
163	    - Return `{"status": "RENEWED", "lease_expires_at": "...", "cancel_requested": false}` on active job.
164	    - Single cancellation channel: renewal response returns `{"status": "RENEWED", "cancel_requested": true, "lease_expires_at": "<current_expiry>"}` when job is in `CANCEL_REQUESTED`, without extending `lease_expires_at`.
165	    - **Heartbeat Cleanup**: Remove `cancelled_job_ids` from worker heartbeat response. Heartbeats remain purely for worker liveness (`{"status": "ACK"}`).
166	    - Expand `POST /v1/workers/{id}/poll` assignment response with `lease_expires_at` and `lease_duration_seconds`.
167	    - Rate Limiting Defense Documentation: renewal flooding is formally recorded as designed, not yet implemented (R-23); planned endpoint defenses are fast state short-circuit, indexed lookup on primary key, and statement timeout; accepted risk under SEC-004.
168	  - Worker Agent (`internal/worker`):
169	    - Dedicated background renewal goroutine per running attempt ticking every 10s (1/3 lease duration; `MINICLOUD_LEASE_DURATION / 3`).
170	    - Renewal response error handling (R-10):
171	      - 200 with `cancel_requested: true`: worker cancels task context, ceases renewals, and still reports its result. If task ignores cancellation, lease expires within 30s and sweeper reaps attempt as `CANCELLED` and job to `CANCELLED` (never `RETRYING`).
172	      - 403 `WORKER_MISMATCH` or 409 (`JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`, `LEASE_EXPIRED`, `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED`): terminal rejection. Immediately abort task, cancel context, and do not report result.
173	      - 404: inspect body. If recognized not-found code (`JOB_NOT_FOUND`, `ATTEMPT_NOT_FOUND`, `WORKER_NOT_FOUND`), terminal rejection: abort task and do not report result. If missing or unrecognized error envelope (old coordinator), log warning once, disable renewals for this attempt, keep running task to completion, and report result via `POST /result`.
174	      - 400 (`INVALID_REQUEST`, `WORKER_TASK_UNSUPPORTED`): log error, stop renewing, keep running task to completion, report result via `POST /result`.
175	      - 5xx / transport error: transient failure. Keep running task, retry renewal next interval until lease expiry.
176	    - Worker result delivery (R-11): 60-second bounded retry loop with exponential backoff on transport errors and ongoing concurrent lease renewals; immediate exit on 4xx terminal rejections (`JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`, `LEASE_EXPIRED`, `WORKER_MISMATCH`, deterministic 400 `INVALID_REQUEST`, 404).
177	  - Coordinator Service (`internal/coordinator`):
178	    - Implement `StartLeaseSweeper(ctx, interval)` ticking every 5s (`MINICLOUD_SWEEPER_INTERVAL`).
179	    - Outage detection (R-09): The sweeper opens an outage grace window of MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW (30 s) at the first successful tick after either (a) any failed sweeper tick, or (b) a database-time gap over 3x the sweep interval between consecutive successful ticks. A lock timeout or SKIP LOCKED skip on a single candidate is not a failed tick. After a window ends, a cooldown of the same length (30 s) begins, during which no new window opens and reaping proceeds, so suppression of reaping never exceeds one window length. The only authoritative grace check is the one inside the sweeper transaction lock.
180	- **Exit Criteria**:
181	  - Long-running jobs (> 30s) maintain continuous lease renewals and commit successfully.
182	  - Cancellation delivered exclusively via renewal response within 10s.
183	  - Result finished during a 45s coordinator/database outage commits within 60s delivery bound once coordinator/database recovers.
184	  - Worker receiving lease loss aborts task and suppresses result reporting.
185	  - Terminating a worker process triggers automated lease expiry and job reclamation.
186	  - Worker renewal handles missing endpoints (old coordinator) and 400 errors without failing the executing task.
187	  - **Mutation Proofs (Mandatory)**:
188	    - Mutating worker renewal response handler to ignore `cancel_requested: true` must cause cooperative cancellation test to fail.
189	    - Disabling worker abort upon renewal rejection must cause worker zombie isolation test to fail.
190	    - Mutating outage gap threshold or cooldown must cause outage grace recovery tests to fail.
191	- **Gating Tests**:
192	  - `TestAPI_RenewLease_SuccessAndStaleRejection` (planned test)
193	  - `TestAgent_PeriodicLeaseRenewal_Success` (planned test)
194	  - `TestAgent_LeaseRenewal_ReceivesCancelRequested` (planned test)
195	  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_ReclaimsAbandonedAttempt` (planned test)
196	  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_InterTickGapExceedsThreshold_EntersGrace` (planned test)
197	  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_InterTickGapBelowThreshold_EntersNoGrace` (planned test)
198	  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_FailedTick_EntersGrace` (planned test)
199	  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_CandidateLockTimeout_EntersNoGrace` (planned test)
200	  - `TestCoordinator_LeaseSweeper_CooldownPreventsConsecutiveGrace` (planned test)
201	  - `TestWorker_Client_RenewOldCoordinatorGracefulDegradation` (planned test)
202	  - `TestWorker_Client_Renew400KeepsRunning` (planned test)
203	  - `TestWorker_Client_ResultDeliveryLoop_BoundedRetry` (planned test)
204	  - `TestWorker_ExecuteAndReport_RetryDuringCoordinatorDowntime_Success` (planned test)
205	  - `TestWorker_ExecuteAndReport_OngoingRenewalWhileRetryingReport` (planned test)
206	  - `TestWorker_ExecuteAndReport_BoundedTimeout_GivesUpAfterLeaseAndGrace` (planned test)
207	  - `TestWorker_ExecuteAndReport_TerminalRejection_HaltsImmediately` (planned test)
208	- **Risks & Mitigations**:
209	  - Renewal goroutine leaks. *Mitigation:* Goroutine terminates unconditionally on attempt context cancellation.
210

213	### Slice 4d: Structured Retries, Failure Classification & Cooperative Cancellation
214	- **Scope**:
215	  - Implement failure classification:
216	    - Success (exit code 0) is `AttemptCompleted` without error classification.
217	    - Validation failures of allowlisted tasks (`echo`, `sleep`, `sha256_loop`) are `ClassPermanent`.
218	    - Worker classifies abnormal terminations (OOM, SIGKILL, context deadline).
219	    - Allowed `error_class` values: `PERMANENT`, `TRANSIENT`, `UNKNOWN` (defaults to `UNKNOWN`).
220	    - `LOST` attempts consume 1 attempt of `max_attempts` budget (`LEASE_EXPIRED`).
221	  - Implement exponential backoff with full jitter ([0.5, 1.5]), base 1s, cap 30s, and calculate `jobs.not_before`.
222	  - Cooperative cancellation:
223	    - Worker agent attaches `context.WithCancel` to executor context.
224	    - Preempted executor returns `context.Canceled`.
225	    - Any commit on `CANCEL_REQUESTED` closes attempt with reported outcome (success moves `CANCEL_REQUESTED -> SUCCEEDED` per today's code in `internal/storage/inmemory.go:615-618` and `internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:1046-1049`, permitted by `internal/domain/transition.go:21`; failure moves `CANCEL_REQUESTED -> CANCELLED`, cancel wins, never `RETRYING`). The coordinator requires no special cancel acknowledgement wire signal.
226	  - Resolve cancellation races (cancel vs completion, cancel vs lease expiry, duplicate cancels).
227	- **Exit Criteria**:
228	  - Transient failures retry up to `max_attempts` with verified backoff delay.
229	  - Permanent failures transition immediately to `FAILED` without retrying.
230	  - Cancelled jobs preempt running workers cooperatively within one renewal interval (10s).
231	  - Zero jobs stranded in `CANCEL_REQUESTED`.
232	  - **Mutation Proofs (Mandatory)**:
233	    - Mutating classification of allowlisted task validation error to `ClassTransient` must cause failure classification test to fail.
234	    - Mutating jitter formula to exceed [0.5, 1.5] bounds must cause jitter distribution test to fail.
235	- **Gating Tests**:
236	  - `TestCoordinator_RetryBackoffWithJitter` (planned test)
237	  - `TestWorker_CooperativeCancellation_ContextPreemption` (planned test)
238	  - `TestAPI_CancelJob_RunningRaces` (planned test)
239	  - `TestExecutor_AllowlistValidation_PermanentFailure` (planned test)
240	- **Risks & Mitigations**:
241	  - Tasks that ignore context cancellation. *Mitigation:* Predefined tasks explicitly inspect `ctx.Done()` on every iteration.
242

### [G-R3-07-accept]
$ git grep -n -i -E 'accept-until-swept|until (it is |the attempt is )?(swept|reaped)|accepted and extended|never reaped while' -- docs ':!docs/agents'

docs/PROJECT_STATE.md:17:  - **Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept Rule (ADR-0014, ADR-0016, `docs/protocol.md`, `docs/state-machine.md`, `docs/failure-model.md`, `docs/fault-injection-plan.md`):** Resolved renewal vs grace design contradiction. Leases grant exclusive rights until swept to `LOST`. While `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`, both `RenewAttemptLease` and `CommitAttempt` succeed even if `lease_expires_at` has passed on database time, extending the lease from current database time (`clock_timestamp() + lease_duration`). Prevents cascading worker aborts during coordinator restarts and database outages. HTTP 409 Conflict (`LEASE_EXPIRED` / `domain.ErrLeaseExpired`) is returned only once an attempt has been swept to `LOST`. Documented trade-off (alive but delayed worker retains attempt until next sweeper pass) and added planned tests.
docs/PROJECT_STATE.md:184:  - Late-commit Option 2: accept-until-swept while attempt is `RUNNING` in database; strictly rejected once swept to `LOST`.
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:85:  5. Attempt state must be `RUNNING`. Under the **Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept Rule**, if `lease_expires_at` has elapsed on database time (for example during sweeper grace periods or ticker intervals) but the sweeper has not yet transitioned the attempt to `LOST`, renewal is accepted and extended from current database time (`clock_timestamp() + make_interval(secs => lease_duration)`), symmetric with commit (except on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job, where the renewal returns the current expiry unchanged without extending the lease). HTTP 409 Conflict (`LEASE_EXPIRED`) is returned only once the attempt has already been swept to `LOST`.
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:203:### 5. Stale Attempt Rejection & Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept Rule
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:207:| Dimension | Option 1: Strict Lease Rejection at Clock Time | Option 2: Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept (Accepted) |
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:214:**Decision**: MiniCloud adopts **Option 2 (Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept while `RUNNING`)** across both `CommitAttempt` and `RenewAttemptLease` (Accepted by owner).
docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md:219:- **Cost of Accept-Until-Swept**: A slow, delayed, or temporarily paused worker that remains alive keeps its attempt even if `lease_expires_at` has passed, until the sweeper runs its next pass. This slightly delays reclaiming stalled workers, but prevents catastrophic cascading aborts during coordinator restarts and database outages, preserving in-progress computation and upholding Phase 3 surviving-worker guarantees.
docs/adr/0016-cancellation-semantics.md:105:  Under the symmetric accept-until-swept rule, renewal is accepted while `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`. HTTP 409 `LEASE_EXPIRED` indicates the coordinator lease sweeper has already swept the attempt to `LOST`.
docs/failure-model.md:11:  3. **Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept**: While `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`, completed work is accepted and renewals are accepted even if `lease_expires_at` has passed on database time, extending the lease from current database time (except on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job, where the renewal returns the current expiry unchanged without extending the lease) and minimizing duplicate execution. Once swept to `LOST`, late commits or renewals are strictly rejected (HTTP 409 `LEASE_EXPIRED` / `domain.ErrLeaseExpired`) and late results are recorded as `DIAGNOSTIC_LATE_RESULT` with **zero resurrection**.
docs/failure-model.md:23:| **FS-03** | **Worker finishes but result is lost/delayed** | Worker retries `ReportResult`. If commit arrives while attempt is still `RUNNING`, it is accepted (symmetric accept-until-swept). If sweeper already swept attempt to `LOST`, commit is rejected with HTTP 409 `LEASE_EXPIRED` (or `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL` if parent job is terminal) and recorded as `DIAGNOSTIC_LATE_RESULT` (no resurrection). | Phase 4 | **Core Phase 4 deliverable:** Rejection of expired attempt commits; diagnostic auditing. |
docs/failure-model.md:33:| **FS-13** | **Worker marked UNHEALTHY completes still-current attempt** | Result accepted if `attempt_id`, `lease_token`, and `worker_id` match and attempt is still `RUNNING` in database (symmetric accept-until-swept). docs/failure-model.md:60:   While the worker's result reporting loop retries, the worker's lease renewal mechanism **continues active lease renewal** (`POST /v1/jobs/{id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/renew`) every 10 seconds. Under the symmetric accept-until-swept rule (ADR-0014), renewals and commits are accepted while `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`. Once the coordinator recovers, renewal extends `lease_expires_at` from database time (except on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job, where renewal returns the current unextended expiry and the worker halts renewals), ensuring the attempt is never reaped while the worker is actively delivering its completed result (on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job, if the lease expires before result delivery completes, the sweeper safely closes the attempt and job as `CANCELLED`).
docs/fault-injection-plan.md:39:- **Expected Outcome**: Worker client exponential backoff retries `ReportResult`. If the commit arrives while the attempt is still in `RUNNING` state (even if `lease_expires_at` passed on database time), it is accepted under the **Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept Rule**, completing the job to `SUCCEEDED` without redundant retries. If the sweeper already swept the attempt to `LOST`, commit is rejected with HTTP 409 Conflict (`LEASE_EXPIRED` / `domain.ErrLeaseExpired`) and recorded as `DIAGNOSTIC_LATE_RESULT` with zero resurrection.
docs/fault-injection-plan.md:61:  - For Sub-scenario B: The worker client retries `ReportResult` with capped, jittered backoff throughout the 45s outage while continuing renewal attempts. Upon coordinator recovery at t = 45s, the sweeper's startup grace period (`MINICLOUD_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD`, 30s) suppresses reaping. Under the symmetric accept-until-swept rule, the worker's queued result commit succeeds with HTTP 200 `COMMITTED`, moving the job to `SUCCEEDED` and preserving Phase 3 guarantees (`TestPostgresWiring_CoordinatorRestartRecovery`) even when execution completes mid-downtime.
docs/phase4-plan.md:11:3. **Late-Commit Semantics**: Option 2 (accept-until-swept while attempt is `RUNNING` in database, even if database timestamp slightly exceeds `lease_expires_at`). Once swept to `LOST`, late commits are strictly rejected with HTTP 409 `LEASE_EXPIRED` or `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED` and zero attempt resurrection.
docs/phase4-plan.md:121:    - `RenewLease`: atomic transaction verifying attempt ownership, active `RUNNING` attempt (symmetric accept-until-swept rule), and constant-time token comparison.
docs/protocol.md:408:*Note on Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept Rule:* While `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`, renewals (`POST /renew`) and commits (`POST /result`) are accepted even if `lease_expires_at` has elapsed on database time (for example during sweeper grace periods or ticker intervals). Renewal extends the lease from current database time (`clock_timestamp() + lease_duration`), except on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job, where the renewal returns the current expiry unchanged without extending the lease. HTTP 409 Conflict (`LEASE_EXPIRED` / `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED` / `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`) is returned only once the attempt has already been swept to `LOST` by the coordinator lease sweeper or the job has reached a terminal state.
docs/state-machine.md:150:- `RUNNING`: Initial state upon pull dispatch. Holds an active lease (`lease_expires_at`). Under the **Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept Rule**, renewals (`POST /renew`) and commits (`POST /result`) are accepted while `state == 'RUNNING'` even if `lease_expires_at` has passed on database time; renewal extends the lease from current database time (`clock_timestamp() + lease_duration`) (except on a CANCEL_REQUESTED job, where the renewal returns the current expiry unchanged without extending the lease). HTTP 409 Conflict (`LEASE_EXPIRED` / `domain.ErrLeaseExpired`) is returned only after the attempt has been swept to `LOST` by the coordinator sweeper.

### [G-R3-07-idem]
$ awk 'NR>=140 && NR<=162 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/adr/0016-cancellation-semantics.md
```
140	#### Race 3: Duplicate Cancellation Requests
141	- **Scenario**: Client issues multiple concurrent or sequential `POST /v1/jobs/{id}/cancel` calls.
142	- **Resolution**:
143	  - Handled directly in the repository `CancelJob` method under the row lock.
144	  - First request transitions job to `CANCELLED` (from `QUEUED` / `RETRYING`) or `CANCEL_REQUESTED` (from `RUNNING`).
145	  - Subsequent requests:
146	    - If job is in `CANCEL_REQUESTED`: Idempotently return HTTP 200 `OK` with `state: "CANCEL_REQUESTED"`, `message: "cancellation processed"` (same body as initial request, no state change, no duplicate event).
147	    - If job is in terminal `CANCELLED` (or `SUCCEEDED`/`FAILED`): Return HTTP `409 Conflict` with `code: "JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL"`.
148	- **Existing Test Impact**:
149	  - Existing tests `CancelJob_QueuedAndRunning` in `internal/storage/storagetest/conformance.go:299-363` (specifically line 335) and `TestInMemoryRepository_SubmitJob_And_CancelJob_Events` in `internal/storage/inmemory_test.go:559` (specifically line 607) test duplicate cancel on terminal `QUEUED -> CANCELLED`, asserting `ErrJobAlreadyTerminal`. Neither test asserts duplicate cancel on `CANCEL_REQUESTED`.
150	  - Conformance plan adds `TestConformance_CancelJob_IdempotentCancelRequested` (planned test) to verify in-memory and PostgreSQL parity for duplicate cancel on `CANCEL_REQUESTED`.
151	
152	---
153	
154	## Consequences
155	
156	### Positive
157	- **Deterministic Bounded Resolution**: Bounded 30s lease sweeps ensure `CANCEL_REQUESTED` always resolves to a terminal state within at most one lease duration (30 seconds) plus sweeper cadence, plus up to one grace window (30 seconds) if an outage happened, even if an uncooperative task ignores context cancellation, without requiring a separate reaper timer.
158	- **Protocol Simplicity**: Eliminating heartbeat cancellation leaves heartbeats focused solely on node health and reduces protocol surface area.
159	- **Idempotency**: Clients retrying cancellation requests on network blips receive safe HTTP 200 responses when retrying a job in `CANCEL_REQUESTED` without corrupting attempt state (a retried cancellation on an already-cancelled job that was `QUEUED` or `RETRYING` returns HTTP 409 `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`).
160	
161	### Negative / Trade-offs
162	- Maximum cancellation delivery latency is 10 seconds (one renewal period).
(exit 0)
```

### [G2c-protocol-renew]
$ awk 'NR>=355 && NR<=425 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/protocol.md
```
355	*Status: Accepted by Owner (2026-10-26, ADR-0014, ADR-0015, ADR-0016)*
356	
357	### 6.1 `POST /v1/jobs/{job_id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/renew` — Attempt Lease Renewal
358	Invoked periodically by the executing worker (default: every 10 seconds) to extend its lease. This is the **sole downstream delivery channel** for cooperative cancellation requests.
359	
360	**Request:**
361	```json
362	{
363	  "worker_id": "worker-1",
364	  "lease_token": "lease_sec_991823..."
365	}
366	```
367	
368	**Response (200 OK — Renewed):**
369	```json
370	{
371	  "status": "RENEWED",
372	  "lease_expires_at": "2026-10-26T20:30:00Z",
373	  "cancel_requested": false
374	}
375	```
376	
377	**Response (200 OK — Cancellation Requested, Lease Not Extended):**
378	```json
379	{
380	  "status": "RENEWED",
381	  "lease_expires_at": "2026-10-26T20:30:00Z",
382	  "cancel_requested": true
383	}
384	```
385	*Note on Cancellation Delivery:* When `cancel_requested` is true, the renewal response returns the current expiry without extending `lease_expires_at`. The worker cancels the task execution context cooperatively via `context.Context`, ceases further renewals, and reports its result outcome. If an uncooperative task ignores context cancellation, the lease expires within at most one lease duration (30 seconds default) from the last renewal, and the coordinator sweeper reaps the attempt, moving the job to `CANCELLED` (never `RETRYING`). Worst-case delivery latency is bounded by exactly **one renewal interval (10 seconds default)**. (Accepted by owner).
386	
387	**Response (403 Forbidden — Worker Mismatch):**
388	```json
389	{
390	  "error": {
391	    "code": "WORKER_MISMATCH",
392	    "message": "worker mismatch: reporting worker is not assigned to attempt",
393	    "details": null
394	  }
395	}
396	```
397	
398	**Response (409 Conflict — Stale Attempt / Lease Expired):**
399	```json
400	{
401	  "error": {
402	    "code": "LEASE_EXPIRED",
403	    "message": "attempt lease expired and was marked lost by sweeper",
404	    "details": null
405	  }
406	}
407	```
408	*Note on Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept Rule:* While `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`, renewals (`POST /renew`) and commits (`POST /result`) are accepted even if `lease_expires_at` has elapsed on database time (for example during sweeper grace periods or ticker intervals). Renewal extends the lease from current database time (`clock_timestamp() + lease_duration`), except on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job, where the renewal returns the current expiry unchanged without extending the lease. HTTP 409 Conflict (`LEASE_EXPIRED` / `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED` / `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`) is returned only once the attempt has already been swept to `LOST` by the coordinator lease sweeper or the job has reached a terminal state.
409	The sweeper opens an outage grace window of MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW (30 s) at the first successful tick after either (a) any failed sweeper tick, or (b) a database-time gap over 3x the sweep interval between consecutive successful ticks. A lock timeout or SKIP LOCKED skip on a single candidate is not a failed tick. After a window ends, a cooldown of the same length (30 s) begins, during which no new window opens and reaping proceeds, so suppression of reaping never exceeds one window length. The only authoritative grace check is the one inside the sweeper transaction lock.
410	
411	*Worker Renewal Response Handling:*
412	- **200 OK (`cancel_requested: true`)**: The worker initiates cooperative task cancellation via `context.Context`, stops renewing the lease, and reports the finished outcome via `POST /result`.
413	- **403 Forbidden (`WORKER_MISMATCH`) or 409 Conflict (`JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`, `LEASE_EXPIRED`, `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED`)**: Terminal rejection. The worker immediately halts task execution, aborts context, frees its execution slot, and **does not report a result** to the coordinator.
414	- **404 Not Found**: The worker inspects the response body error code:
415	  - *Recognized code* (`JOB_NOT_FOUND`, `ATTEMPT_NOT_FOUND`, `WORKER_NOT_FOUND`): Terminal rejection. The worker halts execution, aborts context, and does not report a result.
416	  - *Unrecognized code or missing envelope* (e.g. HTTP 404 from older coordinator lacking the renew route): Treated as endpoint missing. The worker logs a warning once, disables further renewals for that attempt, keeps running the task to completion, and reports the result normally via `POST /result`.
417	- **400 Bad Request (`INVALID_REQUEST`, `WORKER_TASK_UNSUPPORTED`)**: The worker logs an error, disables further renewals for that attempt, keeps running the task to completion, and reports the result normally.
418	- **5xx / Transport / Connection Error**: Transient failure. The worker logs the error, continues task execution, and retries lease renewal on the next interval until the lease expires.
419	
420	---
421	
422	### 6.2 Additive Changes to Existing Endpoints
423	
424	#### `POST /v1/workers/{worker_id}/heartbeat` Response
425	The heartbeat response remains dedicated strictly to node liveness (`{"status": "ACK"}`). Cancellation requests are not delivered via heartbeats (ADR-0016).
(exit 0)
```

### [G2c-adr14-renew]
$ awk 'NR>=78 && NR<=102 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md
```
78	  }
79	  ```
80	- **Validation**:
81	  1. Job must exist and be in `RUNNING` or `CANCEL_REQUESTED`.
82	  2. Attempt must exist, match `job.current_attempt_id`, and be in `RUNNING` state.
83	  3. SHA-256 hash of `lease_token` must match `attempts.lease_token_hash` in constant time (`subtle.ConstantTimeCompare`).
84	  4. Reporting `worker_id` must match `attempts.worker_id`.
85	  5. Attempt state must be `RUNNING`. Under the **Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept Rule**, if `lease_expires_at` has elapsed on database time (for example during sweeper grace periods or ticker intervals) but the sweeper has not yet transitioned the attempt to `LOST`, renewal is accepted and extended from current database time (`clock_timestamp() + make_interval(secs => lease_duration)`), symmetric with commit (except on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job, where the renewal returns the current expiry unchanged without extending the lease). HTTP 409 Conflict (`LEASE_EXPIRED`) is returned only once the attempt has already been swept to `LOST`.
86	- **Response (200 OK)**:
87	  ```json
88	  {
89	    "status": "RENEWED",
90	    "lease_expires_at": "2026-10-26T20:15:30Z",
91	    "cancel_requested": false
92	  }
93	  ```
94	- **Single Cancellation Channel & Commit Outcomes on `CANCEL_REQUESTED`**:
95	  The renewal response is the single channel for delivering cancellation requests to workers. When `job.state == 'CANCEL_REQUESTED'`, the renewal succeeds (`200 OK`), returns `"cancel_requested": true`, and does NOT extend `lease_expires_at` (it returns the current unextended expiry). The worker agent inspects this flag, cancels the task execution context via `context.CancelFunc`, stops further renewals, and still reports its result when execution finishes. If an uncooperative task ignores context cancellation, the lease expires within one lease duration (30s) from the last renewal; the sweeper closes the attempt and the job becomes `CANCELLED` (never `RETRYING`). Worst-case delivery latency is bounded by exactly one renewal interval (10 seconds default). (Accepted by owner).
96	  - When committing a result on `CANCEL_REQUESTED`:
97	    - A success commit (exit code 0 and empty error) transitions attempt to `COMPLETED` and job to `SUCCEEDED`, recording `JOB_COMPLETED`, matching today's verified behavior in `internal/storage/inmemory.go:615-618` and `internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:1046-1049` (permitted by `internal/domain/transition.go:21`).
98	    - A failed commit (non-zero exit code or error string) transitions attempt to `FAILED` and job to `CANCELLED`, recording `JOB_CANCELLED` (cancel intent wins, never `RETRYING`).
99	- **Lease-Lost Handling**: If renewal returns HTTP 409 Conflict (`LEASE_EXPIRED` or `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED`) or 403 Forbidden (`WORKER_MISMATCH`), meaning the attempt was swept to `LOST` by the sweeper, the worker must immediately abort task execution via context cancellation and **must not report a result** to the coordinator. (Accepted by owner).
100	- **Renewal Error Handling & Degradation**:
101	  - **404 Handling**: The worker checks the response body error envelope. If the response contains a recognized JSON error code (`JOB_NOT_FOUND`, `ATTEMPT_NOT_FOUND`, or `WORKER_NOT_FOUND`), the attempt is invalid and the worker aborts immediately. If the 404 response lacks the JSON error envelope (such as a plain text `404 page not found` from an older coordinator missing the `/renew` route), the worker logs once and degrades gracefully, executing to completion without renewals.
102	  - **400 Handling**: On HTTP 400 (`INVALID_REQUEST`, `WORKER_TASK_UNSUPPORTED`), the worker logs an error, ceases renewals, continues task execution to completion, and reports results normally.
(exit 0)
```

### [G2c-fm-renew]
$ awk 'NR>=58 && NR<=80 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/failure-model.md
```
58	   where `base_report_backoff = 500ms`, `max_report_backoff = 5.0s`, and `jitter` in `[0.5, 1.5]`.
59	2. **Concurrent Ongoing Lease Renewal**:
60	   While the worker's result reporting loop retries, the worker's lease renewal mechanism **continues active lease renewal** (`POST /v1/jobs/{id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/renew`) every 10 seconds. Under the symmetric accept-until-swept rule (ADR-0014), renewals and commits are accepted while `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`. Once the coordinator recovers, renewal extends `lease_expires_at` from database time (except on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job, where renewal returns the current unextended expiry and the worker halts renewals), ensuring the attempt is never reaped while the worker is actively delivering its completed result (on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job, if the lease expires before result delivery completes, the sweeper safely closes the attempt and job as `CANCELLED`).
61	3. **Terminal Rejection Halts Delivery**:
62	   On the result reporting path (`ReportResult`), deterministic non-retryable rejections immediately abort the delivery loop without further retries:
63	   - HTTP 400 Bad Request (`INVALID_REQUEST` / payload limit or schema violation)
64	   - HTTP 403 Forbidden (`WORKER_MISMATCH`)
65	   - HTTP 404 Not Found (recognized envelope `JOB_NOT_FOUND`, `ATTEMPT_NOT_FOUND`, `WORKER_NOT_FOUND`, or legacy un-enveloped 404)
66	   - HTTP 409 Conflict (`JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`, `LEASE_EXPIRED`, `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED`)
67	   On the lease renewal path (`RenewAttemptLease`):
68	   - Terminal 409 (`JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`, `LEASE_EXPIRED`, `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED`), 403 (`WORKER_MISMATCH`), and recognized 404 (`JOB_NOT_FOUND`, `ATTEMPT_NOT_FOUND`, `WORKER_NOT_FOUND`) halt renewals immediately.
69	   - HTTP 400 (`INVALID_REQUEST`, `WORKER_TASK_UNSUPPORTED`) or legacy un-enveloped 404 causes the worker to cease renewals while letting the task continue running to completion, reporting the final outcome via `ReportResult`.
70	   - Transient errors (5xx, connection drop) retry on the next renewal interval (10s) until lease expiry.
71	4. **Explicit Delivery Bound**:
72	   The reporting retry loop is bounded by a cumulative deadline of `lease_duration + grace_window` (default: 30s + 30s = 60s), or terminates early if renewal receives HTTP 409 `LEASE_EXPIRED` (indicating the sweeper reaped the attempt).
73	
74	### Existing Test Impact Analysis

76	   - `internal/worker/client_test.go:16-38` (`TestIsNonRetryableReportError`): Currently pins classification of HTTP 403, 404, 409 as terminal/non-retryable and 200, 400, 500, 502, 503, 504 as retryable.
77	   - `internal/worker/client_test.go:40-112` (`TestAgent_ReportResult_RejectionHandling`): Pins single-invocation status code rejection handling.
78	   - `internal/worker/client.go:324-355` (`executeAndReport`): Implements the current 5-attempt retry loop.
79	- **Assertion Impact & Candidate for Owner Approval**:
80	   Expanding `executeAndReport` in `internal/worker/client.go:324-355` from a fixed 5-iteration loop to a 60-second time-bounded loop with capped jittered backoff, and updating `IsNonRetryableReportError` to classify deterministic HTTP 400 as non-retryable (so malformed results do not retry for 60 seconds), is submitted as an additive resilience candidate for owner approval in Slice 4c. Zero assertions in existing tests are weakened or removed without owner approval.
(exit 0)

110	func mapDomainErrorToHTTP(err error) (status int, code string, msg string) {
111		if isConnectionOrTimeoutError(err) {
112			slog.Error("database outage or connection failure", "err", sanitizeError(err.Error()))
113			return http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE", "service temporarily unavailable"
114		}
115	
116		switch {
117		case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrJobNotFound):
118			return http.StatusNotFound, "JOB_NOT_FOUND", err.Error()
119		case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrAttemptNotFound):
120			return http.StatusNotFound, "ATTEMPT_NOT_FOUND", err.Error()
121		case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrWorkerNotFound):
122			return http.StatusNotFound, "WORKER_NOT_FOUND", err.Error()
123		case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrWorkerMismatch):
124			return http.StatusForbidden, "WORKER_MISMATCH", err.Error()
125		case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrStaleAttempt):
126			return http.StatusConflict, "STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED", err.Error()
127		case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrJobAlreadyTerminal):
128			return http.StatusConflict, "JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL", err.Error()
129		case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrUnknownTaskType):

131		case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrPayloadTooLarge):
132			return http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge, "PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE", err.Error()
133		case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrNoQueuedJobs):
134			return http.StatusNoContent, "NO_QUEUED_JOBS", err.Error()
135		case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrNoWorkersAvailable):
136			return http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "NO_WORKERS_AVAILABLE", err.Error()
137		case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrWorkerUnhealthy):
138			return http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "WORKER_UNHEALTHY", err.Error()

140			return http.StatusBadRequest, "INVALID_REQUEST", err.Error()

142			return http.StatusConflict, "JOB_NOT_QUEUED", err.Error()

146			return http.StatusConflict, "WORKER_NO_CAPACITY", err.Error()

151			}
152			slog.Error("internal server error", "err", sanitizeError(err.Error()))
153			return http.StatusInternalServerError, "INTERNAL_ERROR", "internal server error"
154		}
