460	*Allowed `error_classification` values:* `"PERMANENT"`, `"TRANSIENT"`, `"UNKNOWN"` (defaults to `"UNKNOWN"` if omitted on failure). Omitted on successful runs (exit code 0). Note that `"LEASE_EXPIRED"` is an internal failure class assigned exclusively by the coordinator sweeper upon lease expiration and is not accepted in worker result submissions (rejected with HTTP 400 `INVALID_REQUEST`).
461	
462	---
463	
464	### 6.3 Standardized Error Code Additions & HTTP 409 Conflict Evaluation Order
465	When processing attempt results (`POST /result`) and lease renewals (`POST /renew`), conflicting states and validation failures are evaluated in strict priority order:
466	
467	| Priority | Condition | HTTP Status | Error Code | Diagnostic Event Written? | Worker Action |
468	|---|---|---|---|:---:|---|
469	| 1 | Target Job is already in a terminal state (`SUCCEEDED`, `FAILED`, `CANCELLED`) | 409 Conflict | `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL` | Yes (`DIAGNOSTIC_LATE_RESULT`) | Final: Worker halts execution, frees capacity slot, does not report result |
470	| 2 | Attempt state is `LOST` (swept by coordinator lease sweeper) | 409 Conflict | `LEASE_EXPIRED` | Yes (`DIAGNOSTIC_LATE_RESULT`) | Final: Worker halts execution, frees capacity slot, does not report result |
471	| 3 | Attempt state is not `RUNNING`, attempt superseded, or lease token mismatch | 409 Conflict | `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED` | No | Final: Worker halts execution, frees capacity slot, does not report result |
472	| 4 | Reporting worker ID does not match assigned worker | 403 Forbidden | `WORKER_MISMATCH` | No | Final: Worker halts execution, does not report result |
473	| 5 | Target job or attempt does not exist | 404 Not Found | `JOB_NOT_FOUND` / `ATTEMPT_NOT_FOUND` | No | Final: Worker halts execution, does not report result |
474	
475	*Terminal Job Result Rejection Behavior (D-R3-02)*: When a commit (including a retry of a commit that already succeeded) on an already-terminal job is rejected with 409 `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`, the stored result from the initial commit is the single stored result; the retry is rejected; and the worker treats the 409 as final. The repository closes the attempt only if it is still `RUNNING` (and it is the job's current attempt); otherwise no attempt is modified (an attempt already `COMPLETED`, `FAILED`, `CANCELLED` or `LOST` is never overwritten). If the attempt was `RUNNING`, it sets attempt `state = 'CANCELLED'` if `jobs.state == 'CANCELLED'`, or `state = 'LOST'` otherwise, frees the worker capacity slot, and records a diagnostic event (`DIAGNOSTIC_LATE_RESULT`). (Known gap: replaying an identical commit as HTTP 200 is a possible future improvement, not Phase 4).
476	
477	*Commit Outcomes on `CANCEL_REQUESTED` (D-R3-06)*:
478	When committing a result on a job in `CANCEL_REQUESTED`:
479	- A success commit (exit code 0 and empty error) transitions attempt to `COMPLETED` and job to `SUCCEEDED`, recording `JOB_COMPLETED`, matching today's verified behavior in `internal/storage/inmemory.go:615-618` and `internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:1046-1049` (permitted by `internal/domain/transition.go:21`).
480	- A failed commit (non-zero exit code or error string) transitions attempt to `FAILED` and job to `CANCELLED`, recording `JOB_CANCELLED` (cancel intent wins, never `RETRYING` or `FAILED`).
481	- If the lease expires before commit, the sweeper reaps the attempt as `CANCELLED` and job as `CANCELLED`, recording `JOB_CANCELLED` and `LEASE_EXPIRED`.
482	
483	The worker treats all 409 Conflict and 403 Forbidden codes as final terminal rejections. Legacy or redundant codes (`ATTEMPT_NOT_RUNNING`, `JOB_CANCELLED`) are deprecated and consolidated into `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL` (when job is terminal) or `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED` (when attempt is not running).
484	
485	---
486	
487	### 6.4 Backwards Compatibility Matrix & Deployment Policy
488	
489	#### Deployment Rule (Accepted by Owner)
490	MiniCloud V1 coordinator and worker binaries **deploy together as a synchronized release**. Running mixed versions across release boundaries is not a supported long-term operating mode.
(exit 0)

### [G-adr14-sweeper]
$ awk 'NR>=118 && NR<=165 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md
```
118	   FROM attempts a
119	   WHERE a.state = 'RUNNING'
120	     AND a.lease_expires_at < clock_timestamp()
121	   ORDER BY a.lease_expires_at ASC
122	   LIMIT 50;
123	   ```
124	2. **Per-Candidate Atomic Transaction**: For each candidate attempt:
125	   - Acquire row lock on `jobs` first using `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`:
126	     ```sql
127	     SELECT id, state, max_attempts FROM jobs WHERE id = $1 FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
128	     ```
129	     If zero rows are returned, the row is locked by a concurrent transaction or does not exist; the sweeper skips this candidate and retries on the next tick.
130	   - Acquire row lock on `workers` second using plain `FOR UPDATE` under `lock_timeout` (5s):
131	     ```sql
132	     SELECT id FROM workers WHERE id = $1 FOR UPDATE;
133	     ```
134	   - Acquire row lock on `attempts` third using plain `FOR UPDATE` under `lock_timeout` (5s):
135	     ```sql
136	     SELECT id, state, lease_expires_at FROM attempts WHERE id = $1 FOR UPDATE;
137	     ```
138	     If a lock timeout occurs, the transaction rolls back cleanly. (Note: `SKIP LOCKED` and `NOWAIT` are never combined).
139	   - **RE-CHECK UNDER LOCK (Critical Invariant)**:
140	     A concurrent worker renewal (`RenewAttemptLease`) or completion commit (`CommitAttempt`) may have executed between the unlocked candidate scan and row lock acquisition. Furthermore, the only authoritative grace check is evaluated inside the locks: if coordinator startup or outage grace is active, the sweeper skips candidate processing cleanly with zero state mutation. Any earlier check is advisory.
141	     If the coordinator is in a grace window, OR if `attempts.state != 'RUNNING'`, OR if `attempts.lease_expires_at >= clock_timestamp()`, the attempt was concurrently committed, renewed, or protected by grace. The sweeper rolls back or skips the candidate cleanly with zero state mutation. (Accepted by owner).
142	     - Planned test: `TestPostgresRepository_Sweeper_ConcurrentRenewalRecheck` (planned test).
143	   - **State Transitions**:
144	     - **If the job is already in a terminal state** (`CANCELLED`, `SUCCEEDED`, `FAILED`):
145	       - The sweeper transitions the attempt state: to `CANCELLED` if the job is `CANCELLED`, or to `LOST` otherwise.
146	       - The job state is left unchanged (terminal jobs are never mutated).
147	       - The worker capacity slot is released in the same transaction.
148	       - A diagnostic event is recorded (`event_type: 'LEASE_EXPIRED'`).
149	     - **Else if `job.state == 'CANCEL_REQUESTED'`**:
150	       - Transition attempt state to `CANCELLED`.
151	       - Transition job state to `CANCELLED`.
152	       - The worker capacity slot is released.
153	       - Record `job_events` audit record (`event_type: 'JOB_CANCELLED'`).
154	     - **Else** (job is active):
155	       - Transition attempt state to `LOST`.
156	       - Set `finished_at = clock_timestamp()`, `error = 'lease expired: worker failed to renew'`.
157	       - If `attempt_no < job.max_attempts`:
158	         - Transition job to `RETRYING`.
159	         - Compute exponential backoff with full jitter: backoff = min(30s, 1s * 2^(attempt-1)) * jitter, jitter in [0.5, 1.5].
160	         - Set `jobs.not_before = clock_timestamp() + backoff`.
161	       - Else (`attempt_no >= job.max_attempts`):
162	         - Transition job to `FAILED` with error `"max attempts exhausted"`.
163	       - The worker capacity slot is released.
164	       - Record `job_events` audit record (`event_type: 'LEASE_EXPIRED'`).
165

### [G-adr14-sec5]
$ awk 'NR>=203 && NR<=240 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md
```
203	### 5. Stale Attempt Rejection & Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept Rule
204	
205	#### Renewal and Commit Before Sweeper Runs: Option Analysis & Decision
206	
207	| Dimension | Option 1: Strict Lease Rejection at Clock Time | Option 2: Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept (Accepted) |
208	|---|---|---|
209	| **Rule** | `CommitAttempt` and `RenewAttemptLease` reject if `clock_timestamp() > lease_expires_at`, even if `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`. | `CommitAttempt` and `RenewAttemptLease` succeed if `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`, even if `clock_timestamp() > lease_expires_at`, provided the sweeper has not swept the attempt to `LOST`. |
210	| **Costs** | Discards valid computational work completed by a slightly slow or delayed worker, and aborts surviving workers renewing during coordinator restart or outage grace periods. | A slow or lagging worker that is still alive retains its attempt until the sweeper executes its reaping pass, delaying reclamation of a stalled worker until the sweeper runs. |
211	| **Effect on Retries** | Sweeper subsequently reaps attempt to `LOST` and triggers an automated retry. | Avoids redundant retry; job completes directly to `SUCCEEDED` or renewal extends lease from database time. |
212	| **Duplicate Execution** | **Increases duplicate execution**: another worker executes the exact same job even though the first worker is alive and actively computing or renewing. | **Minimizes duplicate execution**: avoids re-running already active or completed tasks when no replacement worker has been dispatched. |
213	
214	**Decision**: MiniCloud adopts **Option 2 (Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept while `RUNNING`)** across both `CommitAttempt` and `RenewAttemptLease` (Accepted by owner).
215	- **Symmetric Rule**: A lease represents exclusive write and execution rights until revoked by the coordinator sweeper. While `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`:
216	  - `CommitAttempt` accepts the result and transitions the job to `SUCCEEDED` (or `FAILED` / `RETRYING`).
217	  - `RenewAttemptLease` accepts the renewal and extends `lease_expires_at` to `clock_timestamp() + make_interval(secs => lease_duration)` (except on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job, where the renewal returns the current expiry unchanged without extending the lease).
218	  Neither commit nor renewal is rejected merely because `lease_expires_at` has passed on the clock while the sweeper was paused for grace or between ticks.
219	- **Cost of Accept-Until-Swept**: A slow, delayed, or temporarily paused worker that remains alive keeps its attempt even if `lease_expires_at` has passed, until the sweeper runs its next pass. This slightly delays reclaiming stalled workers, but prevents catastrophic cascading aborts during coordinator restarts and database outages, preserving in-progress computation and upholding Phase 3 surviving-worker guarantees.
220	- **Code-Level Error Definition & 409 Evaluation Order (D-R3-02)**:
221	  ```go
222	  // ErrJobAlreadyTerminal is returned when an attempt commit is requested on an already-terminal job.
223	  // Today's code returns this directly (internal/storage/inmemory.go:578-579, internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:983-985).
224	  var ErrJobAlreadyTerminal = errors.New("job is already terminal")
225	
226	  // ErrLeaseExpired is returned when a renewal or commit is attempted on an attempt that was already swept to LOST.
227	  var ErrLeaseExpired = errors.New("attempt lease expired and was marked lost")
228	  ```
229	  Evaluation order on `CommitAttempt`:
230	  1. Terminal Job -> HTTP 409 `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`, `domain.ErrJobAlreadyTerminal`. The repository closes the attempt only if it is still `RUNNING` (and it is the job's current attempt); otherwise no attempt is modified (an attempt already `COMPLETED`, `FAILED`, `CANCELLED` or `LOST` is never overwritten). The result row from the first commit is the single stored result; the retry is rejected; the worker treats the 409 as final. (Known gap: replaying an identical commit as HTTP 200 is a possible future improvement, not Phase 4).
231	  2. Swept Attempt (`LOST`) -> HTTP 409 `LEASE_EXPIRED`, `domain.ErrLeaseExpired`.
232	  3. Superseded Attempt / Token Mismatch -> HTTP 409 `STALE_ATTEMPT_REJECTED`, `domain.ErrStaleAttempt`.
233	  The worker treats all three 409 codes as terminal and halts retrying.
234	- **Hard Boundary (No Resurrection Once Swept)**: Once the sweeper acquires row locks and transitions the attempt to `LOST`, Option 2 ceases. Any subsequent commit or renewal attempt is strictly rejected with HTTP 409 Conflict (`LEASE_EXPIRED`) and `domain.ErrLeaseExpired`. At most one `DIAGNOSTIC_LATE_RESULT` audit event is written per attempt (using `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` arbitrated by partial unique index `idx_job_events_unique_diagnostic_late_result` on `(job_id, (details->>'attempt_id')) WHERE event_type = 'DIAGNOSTIC_LATE_RESULT'`) to bound unauthenticated table growth under SEC-004. The result is **never** committed to `results`. Expired attempts are never resurrected. (Accepted by owner).
235	- **Planned Verification Tests**:
236	  - `TestPostgresRepository_RenewAttemptLease_ExpiredButUnswept_RenewedFromDBTime` (planned test).
237	  - `TestPostgresRepository_RenewAttemptLease_AfterSweptToLost_RejectedLeaseExpired` (planned test).
238	  - `TestPostgresRepository_CommitAttempt_ExpiredButUnswept_Accepted` (planned test).
239	  - `TestPostgresRepository_CommitAttempt_AfterSweptToLost_RejectedLeaseExpired` (planned test).
240	  - `TestPostgresRepository_CommitAttempt_RetriedCommitOnTerminalJobLeavesAttemptStateUnchanged` (planned test).
(exit 0)
```

### [G2d-schema]
$ awk 'NR>=360 && NR<=400 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/schema.md
```
360	     AND a.lease_expires_at < clock_timestamp()
361	   ORDER BY a.lease_expires_at ASC
362	   LIMIT 50;
363	   ```
364	   *Why Candidate Scan is Unlocked*: The candidate scan performs an unlocked read against `idx_attempts_lease_expiry` to avoid holding long-lived transaction locks across batch queries. An advisory pre-check before scanning may skip the pass if grace is known to be active, but the authoritative check occurs inside the locks.
365	
366	2. **Per-Candidate Lock Acquisition Order (`jobs -> workers -> attempts`)**:
367	   For each candidate, the sweeper begins an isolated transaction and acquires row locks in canonical order using `SKIP LOCKED` on jobs and plain `FOR UPDATE` under `lock_timeout` on workers and attempts:
368	   ```sql
369	   -- Step 1: Lock parent job row
370	   SELECT id, state, max_attempts FROM jobs WHERE id = $1 FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
371	
372	   -- Step 2: Lock assigned worker row
373	   SELECT id FROM workers WHERE id = $1 FOR UPDATE;
374	
375	   -- Step 3: Lock attempt row and execute correctness re-check
376	   SELECT id, state, lease_expires_at FROM attempts WHERE id = $1 FOR UPDATE;
377	   ```
378	
379	3. **Lock Failure Handling**:
380	   - On Step 1: If the parent job row cannot be locked immediately (e.g. concurrent commit, renewal, or cancel), `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` returns 0 rows. The sweeper skips that candidate cleanly without mutating state, rolls back the transaction, and re-evaluates the candidate on the subsequent sweeper tick.
381	   - On Steps 2 and 3: Worker and attempt rows are locked with plain `FOR UPDATE` under PostgreSQL `lock_timeout` (default 5s). If a timeout occurs, the transaction rolls back cleanly on timeout error and retries next tick. `SKIP LOCKED` and `NOWAIT` are never combined.
382	
383	4. **Re-Check Inside the Locks (Correctness Invariants)**:
384	   Under exclusive row locks, the sweeper performs the single authoritative correctness re-check:
385	   - `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`: Ensures the worker has not already committed.
386	   - `attempts.lease_expires_at < clock_timestamp()`: Ensures a concurrent renewal has not extended the lease.
387	   - **Grace Conditions**: Ensures the coordinator is not within `MINICLOUD_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD` (30s after startup) or `MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW` (30s after database recovery). The only authoritative grace check is this one inside the transaction lock; any earlier check is advisory.
388	   If any condition is not met, the transaction rolls back cleanly with zero mutation.
389	
390	5. **Sweeper State Resolution Rules**:
391	   - **Terminal Job (`SUCCEEDED`, `FAILED`, `CANCELLED`)**: If the parent job is already terminal (for example, if a race occurred or a job was cancelled while the worker was running), the sweeper closes the `RUNNING` attempt without modifying the job state: sets attempt `state = 'CANCELLED'` if `jobs.state == 'CANCELLED'`, or `state = 'LOST'` otherwise. The job state remains strictly unchanged, worker capacity is freed, and a diagnostic job event is recorded (`event_type: 'LEASE_EXPIRED'`).
392	   - **`CANCEL_REQUESTED` Job**: The sweeper closes the attempt as `CANCELLED`, transitions the job to `CANCELLED` (terminal), frees the worker slot, and records a `JOB_CANCELLED` event.
393	   - **`RUNNING` Job**:
394	     - If `attempt_no < max_attempts`: Sets attempt `state = 'LOST'`, `error_class = 'LEASE_EXPIRED'`, sets `jobs.not_before` for exponential backoff with jitter, transitions the job to `RETRYING`, frees the worker slot, and records `LEASE_EXPIRED` and `JOB_RETRYING` events.
395	     - If `attempt_no >= max_attempts`: Sets attempt `state = 'LOST'`, `error_class = 'LEASE_EXPIRED'`, transitions the job to `FAILED` (terminal), frees the worker slot, and records `LEASE_EXPIRED` and `JOB_FAILED` events.
396	
397	### Startup and Outage Grace Mechanisms
398	To protect Phase 3 surviving-worker commit guarantees across coordinator restarts and database outages (`TestPostgresWiring_CoordinatorRestartRecovery`, `TestSecurity_PartD_Outage_MidDispatchAndMidCommit`), the coordinator sweeper suppresses reaping passes for 30 seconds following startup (`MINICLOUD_STARTUP_GRACE_PERIOD`) or database outage restoration (`MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW`). Both periods must be >= lease duration (default 30s).
399	The sweeper opens an outage grace window of MINICLOUD_OUTAGE_GRACE_WINDOW (30 s) at the first successful tick after either (a) any failed sweeper tick, or (b) a database-time gap over 3x the sweep interval between consecutive successful ticks. A lock timeout or SKIP LOCKED skip on a single candidate is not a failed tick. After a window ends, a cooldown of the same length (30 s) begins, during which no new window opens and reaping proceeds, so suppression of reaping never exceeds one window length. The only authoritative grace check is the one inside the sweeper transaction lock.
400	
(exit 0)
```

60	
61	### Reconciling Domain Constants and Database Schema Values
62	There was an initial naming mismatch between the domain constants and PostgreSQL schema constraints:
63	- `internal/domain/state.go:22-27` defined:
64	  `AttemptPending = "PENDING"`, `AttemptRunning = "RUNNING"`, `AttemptCompleted = "COMPLETED"`, `AttemptFailed = "FAILED"`, `AttemptExpired = "EXPIRED"`, `AttemptCanceled = "CANCELED"`.
65	- PostgreSQL schema CHECK constraint `chk_attempts_state` (`000001_initial_schema.sql:97`) defines:
66	  `CHECK (state IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING', 'COMPLETED', 'FAILED', 'TIMED_OUT', 'CANCELLED', 'LOST'))`.
67	
68	### Canonical State Names, Writing Code Paths & Migration Plan
69	MiniCloud establishes the schema values as the canonical state names across the system:
70	- **Canonical Attempt States**: `PENDING`, `RUNNING`, `COMPLETED`, `FAILED`, `TIMED_OUT`, `CANCELLED`, `LOST`.
71	- **Spelling Alignment**: Uses double-L `CANCELLED` across both job and attempt states (`domain.StateCancelled`, `domain.AttemptCancelled`).
72	
73	#### Complete Mapping Table: Domain Constants to Schema Values
74	
75	| Domain Constant (`internal/domain/state.go`) | Constant Value | Schema Enum (`chk_attempts_state`) | Writing Code Path | State Transition Trigger |
76	|---|---|---|---|---|
77	| `AttemptPending` | `"PENDING"` | `PENDING` | `repo.AssignJob` (planned; post-Phase 4 Round-Robin) | Pre-assignment window before worker claims slot |
78	| `AttemptRunning` | `"RUNNING"` | `RUNNING` | `repo.DispatchJob` (`internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:634`) and `repo.DispatchNextJob` (`internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:873`) | Worker claims job via pull dispatch |
79	| `AttemptCompleted` | `"COMPLETED"` | `COMPLETED` | `repo.CommitAttempt` (`internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:946`) | Worker reports exit code 0 and empty error |
80	| `AttemptFailed` | `"FAILED"` | `FAILED` | `repo.CommitAttempt` (`internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:946`) | Worker reports non-zero exit code or task error |
81	| `AttemptTimedOut` (reserved) | `"TIMED_OUT"` | `TIMED_OUT` | Reserved state in schema; not written in V1 | Reserved; worker executor timeout reports `FAILED` with execution timeout |
82	| `AttemptCancelled` (canonical) / `AttemptCanceled` | `"CANCELLED"` | `CANCELLED` | Coordinator Sweeper / `repo.CommitAttempt` | Sweeper reaps attempt on CANCEL_REQUESTED job, or commit on terminal CANCELLED job closes running attempt |
83	| `AttemptLost` (canonical) / `AttemptExpired` (alias) | `"LOST"` | `LOST` | Coordinator Sweeper (ADR-0014) | Lease expires without renewal (`lease_expires_at < clock_timestamp()`) |
84	
85	#### Code & API Mapping Plan
86	In Slice 4a, `internal/domain/state.go` will be updated to declare:
87	```go
88	const (
89	    AttemptPending   AttemptState = "PENDING"
90	    AttemptRunning   AttemptState = "RUNNING"
91	    AttemptCompleted AttemptState = "COMPLETED"
92	    AttemptFailed    AttemptState = "FAILED"
93	    AttemptTimedOut  AttemptState = "TIMED_OUT"
94	    AttemptCancelled AttemptState = "CANCELLED"
95	    AttemptLost      AttemptState = "LOST"
----
136	      [Success Outcome] /       │       \ [Lease Expired / Worker Vanished]
137	                       /        │        \
138	                      ▼         │         ▼
139	                 (COMPLETED)    │       (LOST)
140	                                │
141	          ┌─────────────────────┴─────────────────────┐
142	          ▼                                           ▼
143	   [Task Failure / Worker Cancelled]           [Lease Expired on Cancelled Job]
144	          │                                           │
145	          ▼                                           ▼
146	       (FAILED)                                  (CANCELLED)
147	```
148	
149	- `PENDING`: Staged for post-Phase 4 Assignment-Based Round-Robin slice (Candidate A pre-assignment window).
150	- `RUNNING`: Initial state upon pull dispatch. Holds an active lease (`lease_expires_at`). Under the **Symmetric Accept-Until-Swept Rule**, renewals (`POST /renew`) and commits (`POST /result`) are accepted while `state == 'RUNNING'` even if `lease_expires_at` has passed on database time; renewal extends the lease from current database time (`clock_timestamp() + lease_duration`) (except on a CANCEL_REQUESTED job, where the renewal returns the current expiry unchanged without extending the lease). HTTP 409 Conflict (`LEASE_EXPIRED` / `domain.ErrLeaseExpired`) is returned only after the attempt has been swept to `LOST` by the coordinator sweeper.
151	- `COMPLETED`: Terminal attempt success (exit code 0, empty error).
152	- `FAILED`: Terminal attempt execution failure (non-zero exit code or application error).
153	- `TIMED_OUT`: Reserved for future phase; not written in V1 (worker executor timeout reports `FAILED` with execution timeout error).
154	- `CANCELLED`: Attempt closed by the coordinator sweeper when lease expires on a CANCEL_REQUESTED job, or by CommitAttempt on an already-terminal CANCELLED job closing a RUNNING attempt.
155	- `LOST`: Worker lease expired without renewal and swept by the coordinator sweeper. Consumes 1 attempt of `max_attempts`. Any subsequent commit or renewal attempt is rejected with HTTP 409 Conflict (`LEASE_EXPIRED`) and `domain.ErrLeaseExpired`.
156	
157	---
158	
159	## 5. Lifecycle Audit Events (`job_events`)
160	
161	Phase 4 defines the complete audit event vocabulary stored in `job_events`:
162	
163	| Event Type | Actor | Trigger | Details JSON Payload |
164	|---|---|---|---|
165	| `JOB_SUBMITTED` | Client / API | Job accepted via `POST /v1/jobs`. | `{"task_type": "...", "max_attempts": 3}` |
166	| `JOB_QUEUED` | Coordinator | Job placed into scheduling pool. | `{}` |
167	| `JOB_DISPATCHED` | Worker / Poller | Job claimed via `repo.DispatchNextJob`. | `{"worker_id": "...", "attempt_no": 1, "policy": "fifo_pull"}` |
168	| `LEASE_RENEWED` | Worker / Agent | Worker renewed attempt lease via `/renew`. | `{"attempt_id": "...", "worker_id": "...", "new_expires_at": "..."}` |
169	| `LEASE_EXPIRED` | Sweeper | Coordinator swept expired attempt. | `{"attempt_id": "...", "worker_id": "...", "expired_at": "..."}` |
170	| `JOB_RETRYING` | Coordinator | Retryable failure scheduled for backoff. | `{"attempt_no": 1, "not_before": "...", "backoff_seconds": 2.5}` |
171	| `JOB_CANCEL_REQUESTED` | Client / API | Client requested cancellation for running job. | `{"requested_at": "..."}` |
172	| `JOB_CANCELLED` | Worker / Sweeper | Cancellation confirmed or resolved. | `{"resolved_by": "worker_exit|lease_sweeper"}` |
173	| `JOB_COMPLETED` | Worker | Worker committed successful result. | `{"worker_id": "...", "exit_code": 0}` |
174	| `JOB_FAILED` | Worker / Sweeper | Worker committed permanent error or budget exhausted. | `{"worker_id": "...", "exit_code": 1, "error": "..."}` |
175	| `DIAGNOSTIC_LATE_RESULT` | Worker | Expired worker attempted commit after lease lapse. | `{"reporting_worker": "...", "attempt_id": "...", "rejected": true}` |
176	
177	---
178	
179	## 6. Critical Distributed Invariants
180	
181	1. **Unique Identity:** A job has a stable immutable ID across all retries. Each execution has a distinct `attempt_id` and monotonic `attempt_no`.
182	2. **Commit Authority:** Only the attempt matching the job's current `attempt_id` while still in `RUNNING` state (not yet swept to `LOST`) and presenting the matching `lease_token` can transition the job to `SUCCEEDED` or `FAILED`.
183	3. **Stale Result Rejection:** If an old worker returns a result after its lease expired and was swept to `LOST`, the coordinator rejects the commit with `domain.ErrLeaseExpired` (HTTP 409 Conflict, `LEASE_EXPIRED`). Expired attempts are never resurrected.
184	4. **Single Terminal Commit:** Exactly one terminal outcome is recorded per job in `results`. Once a job reaches `SUCCEEDED`, `FAILED`, `CANCELLED`, or `REJECTED`, all subsequent outcome reports are ignored or logged as diagnostics.
185	5. **Bounded Execution:** Maximum attempts are bounded by `max_attempts` (1..20, default 3). Exceeding `max_attempts` transitions the job permanently to `FAILED`.
186	6. **Lease Expiry Semantics:** A lease expiration proves that the coordinator stopped trusting the worker; it does not prove the worker process halted. Hence, subsequent result reporting by that worker is safely blocked by lease token and attempt state mismatch.
187	7. **Database Clock Authority:** All lease expiration evaluations, worker heartbeats, and retry `not_before` times evaluate strictly on database time (`clock_timestamp()`).
(exit 0)

129	               [Dispatch Job: repo.DispatchNextJob]

341	    ON job_events (job_id, (details->>'attempt_id')) 
342	    WHERE event_type = 'DIAGNOSTIC_LATE_RESULT';

344	
345	### Canonical State Names & Schema Alignment
346	- **Canonical Attempt States**: Migration `000001_initial_schema.sql:97` already defines `chk_attempts_state` with `CHECK (state IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING', 'COMPLETED', 'FAILED', 'TIMED_OUT', 'CANCELLED', 'LOST'))`. No schema migration is required for attempt states.
347	- **State Reconciliation & Mapping**:
348	  - `LOST`: Worker lease expired without renewal (replaces legacy domain `EXPIRED`). Written by sweeper transaction.
349	  - `TIMED_OUT`: Attempt exceeded cumulative `timeout_seconds`. Reserved attempt state in schema; not written by V1 coordinator or worker (worker executor timeout reports `FAILED` with execution timeout error in V1).
350	  - `CANCELLED`: Canonical double-L spelling across both job and attempt states. Written by sweeper (expired lease on a `CANCEL_REQUESTED` job) or commit on an already-terminal `CANCELLED` job closing a `RUNNING` attempt.
351

26	| **FS-06** | **Coordinator crashes while committing result** | Single database transaction provides atomicity across `attempts`, `jobs`, and `results`. 64	
65	### FS-06: Coordinator Crashes After Receiving Result Before/After Committing It
66	- **Planned Test Identifier**: `TestFault_CoordinatorCrash_MidResultCommit` (planned test)
67	- **Target Fault Scenario**: Worker submits execution outcome. Coordinator crashes either: (a) mid-transaction before commit, or (b) immediately after database transaction commits but before HTTP 200 response is transmitted.
68	- **Injection Mechanism**: Injected database failpoint triggers coordinator termination immediately before or after SQL `COMMIT`.
69	- **Expected Outcome**:
70	  - Sub-case A (crash before commit): Database transaction rolls back cleanly. Worker retries `ReportResult` against restarted coordinator; commit succeeds.
71	  - Sub-case B (crash after commit): Database transaction committed. Worker retries `ReportResult` against restarted coordinator; job is already terminal (`SUCCEEDED`), returning HTTP 409 `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`. Single stored result row from the initial commit stands; retry is rejected; worker treats 409 as final without modifying attempt state or creating duplicate result rows (D-R3-02).
72	- **Evidence Collected**: Exactly 1 row in `results` table; 0 duplicate rows; job state `SUCCEEDED`.
73	- **Recovery Mode**: Autonomous (self-healing).
74	
75	### FS-07: PostgreSQL Temporarily Unavailable
76	- **Planned Test Identifier**: `TestFault_PostgresOutage_DuringLeaseExecution` (planned test)
104	  - If commit locks first: job transitions `RUNNING` -> `SUCCEEDED`; subsequent cancel receives HTTP 409 `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL`.
105	  - If cancel locks first: job transitions `RUNNING` -> `CANCEL_REQUESTED` (HTTP 200); worker commit evaluates `CANCEL_REQUESTED`, accepts completed work, and transitions job to `SUCCEEDED` (or confirms cancel to `CANCELLED`).
106	  - If cancel is duplicated on `CANCEL_REQUESTED`: returns idempotent HTTP 200 `CANCEL_REQUESTED` with zero duplicate audit events.
107	  - Cancellation delivered exclusively via renewal response (`cancel_requested: true`).
108	  - Zero deadlocks, zero unhandled errors, exactly one consistent terminal state.
109	- **Evidence Collected**: Terminal state is either `SUCCEEDED` or `CANCELLED`; `job_events` contains sequential audit entries; race detector clean (`-race`).
110	- **Recovery Mode**: Autonomous (self-healing).

### [G2f-findings383]
$ awk 'NR>=376 && NR<=388 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/security/findings.md
```
376	  When worker completes, `CommitAttempt` returns `ErrJobAlreadyTerminal` (`internal/storage/postgres/repository.go:983-984` and `internal/storage/inmemory.go:577-579`) before modifying attempt state, rolling back the transaction. The attempt remains `RUNNING`, holding `available_slots = 0` in `worker_capacity` indefinitely.
377	- **Impact**: Fleet-wide worker slot exhaustion (Denial of Service). Any client reaching the unauthenticated API (SEC-004) can issue two cancellation requests per running job to permanently strand worker capacity. In PostgreSQL mode, this slot leak is durable across coordinator restarts.
378	- **Root Cause**:
379	  1. `CancelJob` omits explicit branch for `CANCEL_REQUESTED`, falling into the `else` branch and prematurely cancelling the job.
380	  2. `CommitAttempt` on a terminal job returns early without closing the attempt and freeing the worker slot.
381	- **Authorized Fix (Scheduled for Slice 4a)**:
382	  1. Handle duplicate cancel on `CANCEL_REQUESTED` as an idempotent no-op returning HTTP 200 with `state: "CANCEL_REQUESTED"` and message `"cancellation processed"` (same body as initial cancel, no status code change).
383	  2. In `CommitAttempt` on a terminal job, close the attempt strictly if still in `RUNNING` (set attempt state to `CANCELLED` if job is `CANCELLED`, or `LOST` otherwise) and release the worker capacity slot in the same transaction as the rejection, record a diagnostic event, and return HTTP 409 `JOB_ALREADY_TERMINAL` (the single stored result stands, an attempt already terminal is never overwritten, and the worker treats HTTP 409 as final without retry).
384	  3. The regression test is the audit probe `TestReviewP400_SEC022_DoubleCancelLeaksWorkerSlot` (audit probe, executed on `agent/claude/P4-00-review`; regression in slice 4a), to be cherry-picked from branch `agent/claude/P4-00-review` in slice 4a and moved into the normal test tree.
385	- **Regression Test**: `TestReviewP400_SEC022_DoubleCancelLeaksWorkerSlot` (audit probe, executed on `agent/claude/P4-00-review`; regression in slice 4a). Conformance test: `TestConformance_CancelJob_IdempotentCancelRequested` (planned test).
386	
387	---
388	
(exit 0)
```

460	//
461	// Precondition Error Precedence (strictly matching PostgreSQL transaction):
462	//  1. Worker existence (ErrWorkerNotFound)
463	//  2. Worker status == HEALTHY (ErrWorkerUnhealthy)
464	//  3. Worker has available capacity (ErrWorkerNoCapacity)
465	//  4. Eligible job exists (ErrNoQueuedJobs)
466	//  5. Job transition validity (ErrInvalidStateTransition)
467	func (r *InMemoryRepository) DispatchNextJob(ctx context.Context, workerID string) (*domain.Job, *domain.Attempt, error) {
468		r.mu.Lock()
469		defer r.mu.Unlock()
470	
471		worker, exists := r.workers[workerID]
472		if !exists {
473			return nil, nil, domain.ErrWorkerNotFound
474		}
475		if worker.Status != domain.WorkerStatusHealthy {
476			return nil, nil, domain.ErrWorkerUnhealthy
477		}
478	
479		availSlots := r.computeAvailableSlotsLocked(worker.ID, worker.TotalSlots)
480		if availSlots <= 0 {
481			return nil, nil, domain.ErrWorkerNoCapacity
482		}
483	
484		// Select next eligible QUEUED job in FIFO order matching worker supported tasks
485		var selectedJob *domain.Job
486		for _, id := range r.jobOrder {
487			j := r.jobs[id]
488			if j != nil && j.State == domain.StateQueued && worker.SupportsTask(j.TaskType) {
489				selectedJob = j
490				break
491			}
492		}
493		if selectedJob == nil {
494			return nil, nil, domain.ErrNoQueuedJobs
495		}
496	
497		// Invariant: At most one active (RUNNING) attempt per job
498		for _, att := range r.attempts {
499			if att.JobID == selectedJob.ID && (att.State == domain.AttemptRunning || att.State == domain.AttemptPending) {
500				return nil, nil, domain.ErrInvalidRequest

### [G3-adr14-bp]
$ awk 'NR>=18 && NR<=28 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/adr/0014-lease-model-and-expiry.md; echo ---- RETRYING in blueprint section 5; grep -n -E 'RETRYING' /tmp/claude-1000/-home-blazeee-MiniCloud-claude/e957ea87-817d-47d8-891e-a15a3d7014b6/scratchpad/bp_5.txt /tmp/claude-1000/-home-blazeee-MiniCloud-claude/e957ea87-817d-47d8-891e-a15a3d7014b6/scratchpad/bp_6.txt | sed -E 's#^.*/bp_([0-9]+)\.txt#page \1#'
```
18	In MiniCloud V1 Phase 3, jobs persist durably in PostgreSQL and workers pull work via atomic database transactions (`repo.DispatchNextJob`, ADR-0011 Option A, ADR-0013). However, Phase 3 explicitly deferred in-flight failure handling:
19	- As documented in `docs/failure-model.md` and `docs/PROJECT_STATE.md`, if a worker process crashes, loses power, or suffers a network partition while executing a task, the job remains in `RUNNING` state indefinitely.
20	- The worker reaper loop (`internal/coordinator/service.go:320-333`) marks inactive workers `UNHEALTHY` when `last_heartbeat_at` exceeds `worker_timeout`, but intentionally leaves active attempts untouched to avoid premature or uncoordinated state mutation.
21	- A partitioned or slow worker may wake up after arbitrary delays and attempt to commit results. Without lease boundaries, late commits could overwrite newer attempts or terminal states.
22	
23	Blueprint Sections 4, 5 ("Job Lifecycle and Consistency Model", pages 5–6), and 10 ("Test Strategy and Failure Injection", pages 9–10) mandate that:
24	1. Every execution attempt runs under an explicit, bounded lease on database time.
25	2. Workers actively renew leases while healthy and executing.
26	3. The coordinator sweeps expired leases and reclaims abandoned jobs.
27	4. Lease expiry represents a loss of coordinator trust in the worker, not physical proof of process termination.
28	5. Late results from expired attempts are strictly rejected for state transitions but retained as diagnostic events.
---- RETRYING in blueprint section 5
page 5:30:    RUNNING             An attempt holds a valid lease.                 SUCCEEDED, RETRYING, FAILED, CANCEL_REQUESTED
page 5:35:    RETRYING            A failed/lost attempt is eligible for another   QUEUED, FAILED, CANCELLED
(exit 0)
```

---- ADR-0016 80-106
78	MiniCloud adopts a single, dedicated delivery channel for cancellation signals:
79	- **Sole Delivery Channel**: `POST /v1/jobs/{id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/renew`.
80	   - While running a task, the worker issues renewal requests every 10 seconds (ADR-0014).
81	   - If `job.state == 'CANCEL_REQUESTED'`, the renewal response returns:
82	     ```json
83	     {
84	       "status": "RENEWED",
85	       "lease_expires_at": "2026-10-26T20:20:00Z",
86	       "cancel_requested": true
87	     }
88	     ```
89	   - **No Extension on Cancel**: On a job in `CANCEL_REQUESTED`, the renewal response returns `"cancel_requested": true` and does **not** extend `lease_expires_at` (it re
90	- **Heartbeat Excluded**: Heartbeats (`POST /v1/workers/{id}/heartbeat`) remain strictly dedicated to node liveness and do not carry `cancelled_job_ids`. (Accepted by own
91	- **Latency Bound**: The worst-case delivery latency from client cancellation to worker receipt is bounded by **one renewal interval (10 seconds default)**. (Accepted by 
92	
93	### 4. Cooperative Worker Cancellation & Lease-Lost Preemption
94	In the worker agent (`internal/worker/client.go` and `internal/executor`):
95	- For every active execution attempt, the worker creates a cancellable execution context:
96	  ```go
97	  taskCtx, cancelFn := context.WithCancel(ctx)
98	  ```
99	- **When `cancel_requested: true` is received on renewal**:
100	  1. The worker agent invokes `cancelFn()`.
101	  2. The task executor receives `taskCtx.Done()`.
102	  3. Predefined tasks (`sleep`, `sha256_loop`, `echo`) observe context cancellation between loop iterations or select blocks, terminate immediately, and return `context.C
103	  4. The worker stops renewing the lease, and reports its result outcome to `POST /v1/jobs/{id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/result`. The coordinator requires no dedicated cance
104	- **When Lease-Lost is received on renewal (HTTP 409 LEASE_EXPIRED or 403 WORKER_MISMATCH)**:
105	  Under the symmetric accept-until-swept rule, renewal is accepted while `attempts.state == 'RUNNING'`. HTTP 409 `LEASE_EXPIRED` indicates the coordinator lease sweeper h
106	  1. The worker agent invokes `cancelFn()`.
---- ADR-0016 132-139
132	  - Seeing `job.state == 'CANCEL_REQUESTED'` and `lease_expires_at < clock_timestamp()`:
133	    1. Sweeper transitions attempt to `CANCELLED`.
134	    2. Sweeper transitions job: `CANCEL_REQUESTED` -> `CANCELLED` (terminal).
135	    3. Sweeper **suppresses retries**: even if `attempt_no < max_attempts`, a cancelled job is never retried (never `RETRYING`).
136	    4. Event: `JobEventCancelled` with detail `{"resolved_by": "lease_sweeper", "reason": "worker unresponsive"}`.
137	  - **Sweeper Rule for Already-Terminal Jobs**:
138	    If the sweeper encounters an attempt whose job is already in a terminal state (`CANCELLED`, `SUCCEEDED`, or `FAILED`), it closes the attempt: to `CANCELLED` if the jo
139

### [G-R3-09-adr15]
$ awk 'NR>=124 && NR<=134 {printf "%d\t%s\n", NR, $0}' docs/adr/0015-retry-policy-and-failure-classification.md
```
124	1. **In-Memory Repository and Queue Dispatch Rule**:
125	   - In both PostgreSQL and in-memory modes, `repo.DispatchNextJob` and `repo.DispatchJob` accept jobs that are either `QUEUED` or in `RETRYING` with `not_before <= now` (where `now` is `clock_timestamp()` in PostgreSQL and the injected `Clock.Now()` in in-memory mode), with zero re-enqueue.
126	   - In Slice 4a, the memory-mode coordinator (`internal/coordinator/service.go`) is rewired to dispatch directly via `repo.DispatchNextJob`, removing the separate queue claiming path and unifying the dispatch architecture across backends. Existing coordinator unit tests that construct `NewServiceWithQueue` are affected: six tests using a spy queue (`internal/coordinator/service_test.go:338-381`) and asserting queue `Ack`/`Release` calls are rewritten to assert observable domain outcomes (job state, attempt, slot), while one test (`TestService_Claim_Redelivery_NoDuplicateAttempt`) using a plain queue plus `q.Enqueue` loses its meaning after rewiring and is rewritten or retired, each marked as a candidate for owner approval.
127	   - In-memory event recording records the actual `from_state` (`QUEUED` or `RETRYING`), and all timestamped mutations evaluate against the injected `Clock`.
128	2. **Shared Conformance Suite Assertions**:
129	   The shared conformance test suite (`internal/storage/storagetest`) asserts identical state machine behavior across both in-memory and PostgreSQL repositories:
130	   - Transitioning a retryable failure to `RETRYING` persists `not_before` in the future.
131	   - Transitioning an attempt failure when `attempt_no >= max_attempts` moves the job to `FAILED` with error `"max attempts exhausted"`.
132	   - Calling `CancelJob` on a job in `RETRYING` state transitions the job directly to `CANCELLED` and clears `not_before`.
133	   - Conformance tests assert dispatch timing: `DispatchNextJob` returns `domain.ErrNoQueuedJobs` (`internal/storage/inmemory.go:494`) when `not_before > now`, and claims the job directly to `RUNNING` once `now >= not_before`.
134	
(exit 0)
```
